关键词：Modern music platforms, Modern music, Modern, heterogeneous audio content, domains

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Modern music platforms expose users to heterogeneous audio content domains such as songs, playlists, and podcasts. Multi-domain sequential recommendation (MDSR) seeks to exploit such cross-domain behavior, yet two obstacles persist in practice. First, joint optimization across heterogeneous domains is prone to negative transfer, with high-traffic domains dominating the shared parameter space and suppressing weaker domains. Second, conventional ID-based collaborative signals fail to bridge domains that are semantically related but exhibit little behavioral overlap. This paper presents CAMAR, a multi-modal causal disentanglement framework for multi-domain audio recommendation. From a causal perspective, user behavior is modeled as the joint outcome of a domain-invariant preference and domain-specific preferences. Guided by this view, CAMAR factorizes user representations into domain-invariant and domain-specific components and enforces domain adversarial invariance together with orthogonality, restricting cross-domain interaction to the invariant subspace and suppressing harmful transfer paths. To strengthen cross-domain semantic coupling, CAMAR derives trainable semantic-ID embeddings from pre-trained text, vision, and audio features through residual quantization, yielding an end-to-end multi-modal adaptation tailored to recommendation. A transfer network imputes user representations for inactive domains, mitigating cross-domain cold start. Experiments on both public and industrial datasets demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests on NetEase Cloud Music, one of China's largest music streaming platforms, CAMAR delivers consistent gains across all three domains in their main traffic scenarios, validating its effectiveness in multi-domain audio recommendation at scale.

11. 【2611.33627】Welfare-Enhancing Mechanism Design for Agent Ecosystems

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33627

作者：Zitian Guo,Chen Xu,Beibei Li,Chenyan Xiong

类目：Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Computer Science and Game Theory (cs.GT); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：decentralized service market, emerging Agentic Web, Agentic Web forms, user agent selects, Agentic Web

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The emerging Agentic Web forms a multi-agent ecosystem: a decentralized service market where the user agent selects which content agents to query from their submitted profiles, before seeing any documents. Profiles therefore become a new target of optimization. Existing Generative Engine Optimization studies how a single supplier rewrites documents the engine can already retrieve, to improve their citation. In the Agentic Web, multiple content agents optimize profiles to compete for limited opportunities to deliver documents. To study this repeated interaction, we build WildRegistry, a simulation framework and benchmark based on a real agentic-search request stream, and model it as a dynamic game under private monitoring. Long-horizon experiments show that this competition lowers website-selection quality and settles at a harmful stable point, which our empirical criterion identifies as an approximate rewriting equilibrium. We therefore design a welfare-enhancing mechanism, DEC (Delivery-Evidence Credit), a platform-side policy that uses recorded delivery outcomes to reallocate later opportunities and reduce information asymmetry. DEC improves this endpoint, raising website-selection quality while preserving most of providers' exposure gains, for a Welfare Gain of +2.01 points and a healthier long-horizon outcome. More broadly, our findings highlight the importance of mechanism design in agent ecosystems: turning recorded delivery outcomes into evidence that endogenously shapes later user-agent selection and content-agent adaptation can steer long-horizon adaptation toward delivered capabilities.

12. 【2611.33546】Community Notes Across Platforms: Evaluating the Feasibility of Re-using Crowd-sourced Context Notes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33546

作者：Nishka Arora,Yann Issam Aouidef,Ashish Goel

类目：ocial and Information Networks (cs.SI); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Community Notes, Notes, Crowdsourced fact-checking systems, Bluesky, Community

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Crowdsourced fact-checking systems such as Community Notes can provide useful context for misleading content. Producing and rating new notes on such systems requires substantial contributor effort. However, the resulting context is typically confined to the post and platform for which it was created. We ask whether already-vetted notes can be reused when the same claims recur across posts and platforms. To investigate this question, we construct a human-labelled dataset using Community Notes attached to X posts created during a one-week period and candidate posts retrieved from Bluesky. Within our observation window, 140 of 340 claims had at least one confirmed recurrence on Bluesky, showing that cross-platform note reuse is a substantial opportunity. Recurrence alone, however, does not establish whether reuse would provide timely context. A timing analysis shows that many relevant notes were already Helpful when matching Bluesky posts appeared. Even when a Bluesky post appeared first, reuse could often still provide context sooner after publication than the original X post received its Helpful note. To automate the discovery of these opportunities, we evaluate a three-stage pipeline that searches the Bluesky stream for potential recurrences and determines whether an existing note applies. The pipeline achieves 72.6% precision in a held-out stream evaluation and 70.1% end-to-end recall on our known-positive set. We further characterize the pipeline's costs and principal failure modes. Crossnote, our prototype Bluesky labeler, implements this approach by applying existing Community Notes to matching posts. Our findings suggest that reusing vetted crowdsourced context is a compelling way to scale the reach and impact of Community Notes. Note reuse can complement the production of new notes by reducing duplicated effort and shortening time to context when misleading claims recur.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33497

作者：Vimal Kumar Kumar,Meghana Kshirsagar,Conor Ryan

备注： 18 pages (including appendix), 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.

On eight public datasets from five platforms, we compare four structural predictors, which use nothing about the learner beyond the last activity, with ten personalised recommenders and with open-weight LLM rankers from 0.5 to 7 billion parameters, under two protocols. In a simulation with known learner needs, the structural share tracks the share of steps decided by the platform, and offline accuracy ranks an oracle of the learners' needs below imitators of the platform. Weighting test steps by how unlikely the structure makes them restores much of the link with usefulness and, on the real data, reveals a significant personalisation signal on seven of the eight datasets. We propose a syllabus-aware evaluation checklist and release a Python package that reports the lift of any recommender over the structure.

1. 【2611.34944】AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://weijielyu.github.io/AnyCam/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Camera-controlled video generation re-renders a video along a target camera trajectory, yet moving the camera around an object, the most natural way to change the viewpoint of an object-centric scene, remains difficult. Existing methods condition either on camera parameters, which are scale-ambiguous in monocular video, or on explicit geometry such as warped point clouds or depth, which breaks down under large viewpoint changes. We present AnyCam, a method that represents the camera with an anchor object in the scene: we reconstruct it in 3D and render it along the target trajectory, a camera signal that is scale-aware, view-complete and easy to specify relative to the object. A video diffusion model transfers the content and dynamics of the source video to the new viewpoint. Its H-Concat layout aligns source and target frames in time, so the model can be distilled into AnyCam-AR, a streaming few-step generator for on-the-fly camera control. Both models are trained on paired videos with pixel-aligned anchor renderings from multi-camera videos and static-scene captures. Experiments on diverse datasets and trajectories show that AnyCam performs favorably against camera-controlled video generation and 3D reconstruction methods in camera control and visual quality.

Abstract:A general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) has surpassed expert vision models in many domains such as object detection and video recognition. However, point correspondence is known to be challenging for VLMs since it goes beyond object-level recognition: it requires pixel-level localization of the same physical surface point across observations, even when appearance changes or the point disappears and later reappears. In this paper, we introduce AgenTAP, a VLM-based agent that directly predicts point coordinates and visibility and actively reasons over videos. We further use AgenTAP to re-identify physical points after disappearance and reconnect fragmented tracks, constructing EgoTAP-4D, a dataset of 3D point trajectories in long real-world egocentric videos. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image editing models can follow a wide range of instructions, but video editing models still lag behind, limited in part by the difficulty of collecting paired source and edited videos. Is it possible to transfer advanced image editing capabilities to video editors without new paired videos? It enables efficient post-training with no architectural changes and no new videos, using only individual images. Although the video editor sees only single images during training, at test time it applies the improved and expanded capabilities to whole video clips while preserving temporal consistency. To push the boundaries of video editing, we introduce OpenVE++, an extension of OpenVE-Bench that adds four new editing tasks useful for video production, including action changes, expression changes, body movements, and scene relighting. 4. 【2611.34940】LuxAvatar: Real-Time Relightable Head Avatars via Causal Neural Shading of 3D Intrinsic Proxies

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940

作者：Yipengjing Sun,Chenyang Wang,Boyao Zhou,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Shengping Zhang

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers. Conditioned on these buffers, our neural shader formulates forward rendering as a conditional video diffusion process, bypassing explicit PBR computation to synthesize realistic shading effects under target illumination. We achieve real-time streaming through heterogeneous distillation from a pretrained multi-step bidirectional relighting teacher to a two-step causal autoregressive neural shader, inheriting broad synthetic lighting priors while minimizing conditioning and decoding latency. To further specialize these priors to human heads, we construct a hybrid relighting dataset for domain-specific fine-tuning, capturing authentic facial dynamics and physically grounded light transport. Extensive experiments show that our framework enables 3D-controllable, real-time portrait animation and relighting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity, motion control, and lighting accuracy.

5. 【2611.34939】A Mesh for Every Moment

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939

作者：Roy Or-El,Jeong Joon Park,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：multiple synchronized cameras, dynamic Gaussian splatting, Gaussian splatting methods, Gaussian, captured by multiple

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://memo-4d.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present MEMO (a Mesh for Every MOment), a method that reconstructs the geometry of complete dynamic scenes, captured by multiple synchronized cameras, and extracts a mesh at every frame. Existing dynamic Gaussian splatting methods either model appearance alone or target isolated objects segmented by foreground masks, and all of them recover meshes by TSDF fusion or Poisson reconstruction, whose fixed grids limit the detail they can capture. At the core of MEMO is a per-Gaussian motion model inspired by Gaussian processes, which represents each Gaussian's position and rotation over time as a combination of temporal Gaussian kernels. The model keeps every Gaussian a valid surface element throughout the sequence and carries an implicit representation of the dynamic geometry, so a mesh can be derived at any time from a field defined by the Gaussians. Although common in real captures, large and fast motion poses a challenge that dynamic Gaussian splatting methods usually overlook. To account for it, we design a track-based initialization scheme that fits each Gaussian's initial trajectory to point tracks in closed form. We evaluate MEMO on Blender-Moments, a new benchmark of nine animated scenes with ground-truth meshes at every frame, and on two real-world datasets. MEMO reconstructs the most accurate geometry among state-of-the-art methods, and remains competitive in novel-view synthesis and training time. Project page: this https URL

Abstract:Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. 7. 【2611.34931】DynMF2: Rigid Motion Factorization by Expectation-Maximization for Monocular 4D Reconstruction and Motion Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34931

作者：Agelos Kratimenos,Yile Hua,Kostas Daniilidis

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：single casual video, casual video leaves, match every frame, single casual, casual video

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A single casual video leaves a moving scene under-determined: a point seen once from each place can slide toward or away from the camera and still match every frame. We constrain the reconstruction with the structure of the motion itself. Building on DynMF, we model a scene's motion as a handful of SE(3) trajectories, each rigid, and let every point follow one of them alone, never a blend. DynMF2 computes this factorization directly from 3D point trajectories, with no rendering, by Expectation-Maximization (EM). Its M-step fits, in closed form, every rigid motion to the points assigned to it, and its E-step reassigns every point to the motion that explains it with the least error. The background is simply one more motion, the camera's, so no masks are needed. Three tools then revise the factorization: requery adds trajectories where the rendering shows parts the tracker missed, merge joins motions that move together, and split separates those that do not. Thus the number of motions follows the scene. Without masks, DynMF2 clearly outperforms the mask-based baselines in view synthesis on iPhone and NVIDIA and in 3D tracking on iPhone. It recovers what masks leave out, from thin parts and details to small movers far in the background. Its motions segment the moving parts of MOVi-F and iTACO scenes competitively, although DynMF2 is never trained to segment. What moves in a scene is not decided in advance: it emerges from the rigid motions themselves.

8. 【2611.34929】High-Fidelity Image Matting with Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929

作者：Baihan Yang,Xin Lin,Yufan Wei,Baiang Li,Yuheng Liu,Juanxi Tian,Xiaohui Xie,Truong Nguyen

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone's native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. With pixel-accurate synthetic supervision and real-image fine-tuning, PixelMatte predicts alpha mattes in a single forward pass. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: this https URL.

9. 【2611.34914】Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914

作者：Agelos Kratimenos,Kostas Daniilidis,Evangelos Chatzipantazis

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：capable low-level controllers, low-level controllers, capable low-level, wrong place, VLA

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA's intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA's denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object's poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA's weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen {\pi}0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a {\pi}0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID {\pi}0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm's reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

10. 【2611.34912】LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912

作者：Jiachen An,Sijie Zhao,Le Li,Hongyu Liu,Zhiyuan Li,Tianyu Yang,Xiaoyu Kong,Xunliang Cai,Yong Zhang,Chi-Man Pun,Xiaoming Wei,Xiaodong Cun

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：primary latency bottleneck, video VAE remains, generation and editing, increasingly streamable, remains a primary

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://livevae.github.io/) Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/MeiGen-AI/LiveVAE)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

11. 【2611.34907】Agentic LongLive: Asynchronous Agentic Memory for Long Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907

作者：Shaoteng Liu,Shuai Yang,Wei Huang,Luozhou Wang,Song Han,Yukang Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：long interactive story, interactive story, generators stream minutes, places consistent, Real-time autoregressive video

备注： 28 pages, 12 figures. Project page: [this https URL](https://nvlabs.github.io/LongLive/Agentic-LongLive/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. From this entity memory it composes a global image and a per-shot memory image, which an auxiliary GPU prefills into the generator's attention sink before the next shot starts, and writes entity cards for the prompts that name them, so the video never waits for memory and video errors are not written back. A small language model, trained with reinforcement learning and an image reward calibrated using downstream video outcomes, decides what to remember before the next prompt is known. On LongStory-Online, a new benchmark of 25 stories (857 shots) revealed one shot at a time, Agentic LongLive improves persistent-state accuracy of LongLive-2.0, its base generator, by 26.3 points, protagonist identity by 44.9, and revisited-place consistency by 40.4, at about 1% extra generation time on H100. The full system streams faster than real time on one 8xB200 node with the generator in BF16 (34.3 FPS), keeps image quality stable over 14-minute stories, and improves identity on two MiniMax-H3 generators, one of which, LongLive-H3, also runs in real time with it. To our knowledge, it is the first real-time video generator with text and image memory kept by an LLM agent.

12. 【2611.34903】ProbFaS: Probabilistic Video Frame Selection for MLLMs via Multi-Fidelity Calibration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34903

作者：Tianru Zhang,Mayank Nautiyal,Hugo Eidmann,Prashant Singh,Ekta Vats

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Processing long videos, large language models, multimodal large language, Processing long, accurate video understanding

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Processing long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is prohibitively expensive, making informative frame selection essential for accurate video understanding. The default uniform sampling often misses task-relevant keyframes, while recent training-free selectors instead pick frames by how well each one matches the question under an image-text alignment (ITA) score. However, we show that ITA signals correspond poorly with the MLLM's true needs: their rankings correlate weakly with downstream accuracy and are even negatively correlated on over 40% of questions, whereas the MLLM's internal yes/no judgment yields a much stronger signal. We present ProbFaS, a training- and label-free probabilistic pipeline that fuses both signals for key frame selection. ProbFaS treats dense ITA scores as cheap, biased measurements and sparse MLLM judgments as accurate, costly ones, unifying them via co-kriging, a multi-fidelity Gaussian process, to calibrate relevance across all frames. A temporal determinantal point process then selects frames capturing relevance peaks while preserving temporal coverage. Across Video-MME, MLVU and LongVideoBench, ProbFaS is the most accurate among fourteen state-of-the-art training-free selectors and uniform sampling in 13 of 15 settings (8-128 frames), outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 3.1 percentage points. On MLVU and LongVideoBench, 32 ProbFaS frames match or surpass the top baseline at 128 frames and uniform sampling with up to 768 frames.

14. 【2611.34894】What a Drop Reveals: Sim-to-Real Material Estimation for Deformable Objects from Video

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34894

作者：Ziyue Peng,Yanxi Hu,Junyoung Kim,Andrew Chen,Jin Yang,Chandrajit L. Bajaj,Etienne Vouga,William T. Freeman,Qixing Huang,Brandon Y. Feng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：hits the ground, ground reveals, object deforms, simulation, real

备注： 74 pages. Project page: [this https URL](https://bjxdw.github.io/PhysReader/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:How an object deforms when it hits the ground reveals what it is made of. From a single real video of a drop, we estimate a deformable object's density-normalized stiffness $E/\rho$, and its yield stress $\sigma_Y/\rho$ when the impact causes plastic deformation, with a feed-forward predictor trained only in simulation. Real videos carry no material labels, so such predictors are developed and validated on synthetic data alone. We show that synthetic validation ranks their geometric inputs in the wrong order. Material acts through 3D deformation, and tracklets lifted to 3D with estimated depth are indeed the most accurate input in simulation. However, on real drops they are the least accurate, and better depth does not repair them: the failure persists with simulator depth in training, LiDAR depth at test time, and exact depth on rendered replays of the real drops. Short 2D optical-flow tracklets do the opposite: they improve nothing in simulation, yet they transfer best. Material is therefore better read from what perception measures consistently in both domains than from what is most informative in simulation. Our predictor, PhysReader, reads the video and 2D tracklets and learns the lift to 3D in simulation by matching a teacher that sees exact 3D deformation. To measure transfer, we introduce PhysReader-Real: 16 everyday objects dropped five times each, with reference materials fit jointly to each object's drops and checked against tensile tests. On PhysReader-Real, PhysReader is closer to the reference materials than every other input and baseline. The one exception is a per-scene optimizer started from PhysReader's prediction, which ends slightly closer but takes 49 minutes per video instead of 12 seconds.

16. 【2611.34881】Stranger Things: When Objects Appear Without Their Typical Neighbors

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34881

作者：Siddhartha Gairola,Jiahao Xie,Anna Kukleva,Francesco Locatello,Bernt Schiele

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：scenes inform, powerful cue, Grounded Evaluation Benchmark, Visual context, typicality bias

备注： This paper was accepted to the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Track on Evaluations and Datasets. Dataset: [this http URL](http://huggingface.co/datasets/sidgairo18/strange-bench)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual context is a powerful cue for object recognition: scenes inform what, where, and at what scale objects are likely to appear. Yet over-reliance on context becomes a failure mode when models predict objects based on their typical neighbors rather than the objects' own visual features, which we call typicality bias. While extensively studied in the pre-Transformer era, it has often been assumed that architectural and scale improvements would naturally solve it, and a systematic diagnostic across the modern vision stack remains absent. We provide such a diagnostic framework. We measure typicality bias via normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI) over object pairs, and introduce STRANGE-Bench (Synthetic Typicality-RANked Grounded Evaluation Benchmark): a photorealistic, layout-controlled benchmark spanning 17 sub-tiers from typical to never-co-occurring object pairs. Across five model families (specialist detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, self-supervised, vision-language, and multimodal large language models) and three recognition tasks (supervised detection, open-vocabulary detection, and classification), the bias is prevalent: gaps of 5-11 p.p. persist when objects appear outside their typical company, and do not consistently shrink with scale. To mitigate it, we turn to data augmentation: existing augmentations are designed for visual diversity, not for breaking co-occurrence shortcuts, and sample from the very distribution that produces typicality bias. We propose Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS), a simple sampling rule for paste-based augmentations that uses the NPMI signal to tilt the training-time co-occurrence distribution toward atypical partners. AAS reduces the typicality gap by up to 1.6 p.p. on natural-image COCO splits and 23.0 p.p. on synthetic STRANGE-Bench across three model families, without sacrificing overall performance.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877

Abstract:Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. 20. 【2611.34860】DocDUR-Net: Degradation-Aware Unified Document Image Restoration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34860

作者：Haowei Xu,Peirong Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Captured document images, uncontrolled imaging conditions, fine text structures, obscure fine text, diverse degradations caused

备注： Accepted to ICIG 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Captured document images often suffer from diverse degradations caused by uncontrolled imaging conditions, which reduce readability and obscure fine text structures. Single-degradation restoration methods are usually trained and deployed for individual degradation types, and therefore require model selection before restoration. Unified restoration models alleviate the need to maintain multiple restoration networks by sharing one network across tasks, but representative unified document image restoration methods still depend on external task selection. This dependency limits automatic document image restoration. We propose DocDUR-Net, a degradation-aware unified restoration network that derives restoration guidance directly from the input image. It introduces a Degradation Perception Module (DPM) to extract degradation representations and predict degradation weights, and Degradation Prior-guided Adaptive Feature Fusion (DP-AFF) to combine learnable degradation priors with degradation features extracted from the input. The resulting guidance is injected into a shared restoration backbone, enabling adaptive restoration without external task selection. DocDUR-Net also supports a manual mode with explicit task selection. Experiments on document image restoration benchmarks show that DocDUR-Net achieves the best average PSNR of 29.405 dB and SSIM of 0.960 among unified methods while remaining competitive with single-degradation methods.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spike cameras capture high-speed motion through an integrate-and-fire mechanism, providing fine-grained temporal observations without exposure blur. However, their binary streams encode accumulated illumination rather than instantaneous intensity, making reliable motion estimation challenging. Existing spike-based methods exploit temporal aggregation to construct motion representations, but this process may weaken fine-grained firing patterns. Meanwhile, correlation-based estimators often rely on learned matching features without explicitly characterizing the spatial distribution and ambiguity of candidate displacements. To address these challenges, we propose TIDE-Flow, a spike-based optical flow estimation framework that jointly exploits Temporal Integration Evidence (TIE) and Displacement Evidence Encoding (DEE). TIE combines raw spike patterns, multi-scale firing statistics, temporal moments, and boundary-aware interval cues, preserving complementary temporal information before spatial feature compression. DEE further integrates structured candidate-grid features with explicit displacement statistics to characterize matching geometry and competing correspondences. Both components are incorporated into a difference-correlation recurrent refinement framework, enabling effective temporal motion modeling and correspondence estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of TIDE-Flow, with controlled ablations validating the contributions of temporal and displacement evidence modeling.

22. 【2611.34849】Real2SimLab: A Unified Platform for Real-to-Simulation Transfer

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34849

作者：Yongchang Zhang,Jie Huang,Yunhe Ni,Ningyu Zhou,Guangyu Zhao,Ruixun Liu,Rui Huang,Yidi Wu,TianZe Zhang,Zixiang Jiang,Xiang Li,Kevin Shen,Yiwu Zhong

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：real-world observations, long been studied, benchmarks, pipeline, Abstract

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://real2simhub.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-to-simulation transfer, which builds simulations from real-world observations, has long been studied in research and industry. Recently, coding agents have become capable of generating simulation programs from video recordings, and several benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate them. However, each individual benchmark defines its own inputs, deliverables, and scoring pipeline. This leads to $N \times M$ integrations when evaluating $N$ methods on $M$ benchmarks. To this end, we present Real2SimLab, a unified platform for real-to-simulation transfer. It evaluates a method by running its written program and scoring the output simulation with the original scorer of each benchmark. Moreover, Real2SimLab provides a pipeline to create training data and reward annotations for real-to-simulation methods. Our pipeline carefully avoids data contamination by filtering out the video recordings that exist in benchmarks. For each video recording, a program is first produced, and then our pipeline computes its reward with the same code that Real2SimLab uses for evaluation. So far, it supports 47 coding agents and 5 non-agent methods on 6 benchmarks. Both the platform and Awesome-Real2Sim, our research map of nearly 300 real-to-simulation papers, are open source, and we invite the Real2SimHub community to build them together.

23. 【2611.34843】Probing Visual Idiosyncrasies in Text-to-Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34843

作者：Yongqian Peng,Hongjie Li,Yichao Zhou,Chi Zhang,Yixin Zhu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：turn short text, short text descriptions, models turn short, comparable visual quality, turn short

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-Image (T2I) models turn short text descriptions into images, and the leading models now produce images of comparable visual quality. Whether their outputs still carry model-specific characteristics matters for attribution, for model comparison beyond aggregate quality, and for evaluations that assume anonymity. We ask whether images from frontier T2I models can still be classified by source, and which visual properties make this possible. Here we show that a standard ResNet-50 identifies the source of an image among HiDream-O1-Dev, FLUX.2-dev, HunyuanImage-3.0, and Qwen-Image-2512, the four highest-ranked open-source models on the Text-to-Image Arena, with 100.00% accuracy on held-out prompts. Human participants reach only 62.61% on the pairwise version of this task, so the distinguishing cues are not visually obvious. They are also not tied to a particular classifier and survive severe image corruptions and out-of-distribution prompts. We trace them to visual idiosyncrasies at three levels. Low-level texture statistics alone let a lightweight multi-layer perceptron match ResNet-50. Object layout, depth, and albedo estimated from the images each carry a source signal. Image captions reveal model-specific preferences in style and background that the prompts never specify. A one-sentence hint about these cues raises human accuracy by 9.03 percentage points. These idiosyncrasies make source classification a tool for measuring similarity between models and between real and generated images, and they caution against assuming anonymity in human preference evaluation of T2I models.

24. 【2611.34839】Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839

作者：Abinitha Gourabathina,Sana Tonekaboni,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Akbar Rafiey

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：omit requested objects, satisfy complex prompts, requested objects, satisfy complex, omit requested

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34828

作者：Hanwen Liang,Wenxuan Liu,Wenxin Huang,Jingling Yuan,Zhaofei Yu,Xian Zhong,Tiejun Huang

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Underexposure weakens object details, and overexposure obscures boundaries. Spike cameras encode light into high-frequency binary streams. Long-window spike counts give stable appearance cues, while short observations reveal changes in firing rates from sparse measurements. We propose SPIRE, a sensor-grounded semantic-spiking framework for illumination-robust object detection from a single spike stream. SPIRE builds an appearance view from the full packet through detection-oriented illumination adaptation and constructs a sequence of cumulative views from progressively longer prefixes. At selected scales, ANN features and spike-derived initialization jointly set the initial membrane potentials and firing thresholds, allowing semantic cues to shape the responses during temporal encoding. Local spike-count contrasts supervise both branches during training. On simulated VOC and COCO, SPIRE-D outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.00-5.50 AP50 points across the four under- and overexposure settings. 26. 【2611.34813】Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813

作者：Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Haotian Zhang,Zehong Zhao,Ran Ji,Siyuan An,Huayi Qin,Qingying Gao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziqi Huang,Ruisi Wang,Maijunxian Wang,Hokin Deng

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810

作者：Yuyang Yin,Chenjie Cao,Xuhui Zuo,Yuning Gong,Pengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Zhenwei Wang,Yao Zhao,Tengfei Wang,Yunchao Wei,Chunchao Guo

Abstract:Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

29. 【2611.34801】Guidance as Variance Matching

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801

作者：Ali Backour,Artem Lukoianov,Frank Permenter,Tommi Jaakkola,Justin Solomon,Vincent Sitzmann

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：weaker reference prediction, weaker reference, Guidance, reference prediction, prediction

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model's training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior's covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

Abstract:Existing Knowledge Distillation (KD) methods for semantic segmentation often guide the student to mimic the teacher's structured information generated from an individual image. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34782

备注： 14 pages, 7 figures, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pretrained visual representations accelerate generative learning by abstracting away appearance and spatial detail. Yet the same abstraction makes the return to pixels far harder, shifting much of the burden onto a larger decoder. We ask why the representation-to-pixel path should be learned independently when the encoder already provides a learned pixel-to-semantics path (the semantic encoding path). We introduce RevFlow, based on the principle of generation as reverse encoding. RevFlow uses intermediate representations of a frozen encoder as successive generative targets, first generating deep semantic representations, then shallow representations that retain appearance and spatial detail, before decoding to pixels. To learn these transitions without training a separate generator for each stage, we develop HyC-DiT, a unified generative model with a standard DiT backbone shared across stages and stage-specific condition injection. We further show that native matching in semantic representation space enables few-step generation. RevFlow accelerates both learning and sampling. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, RevFlow reaches 1.96 FID at 690M and 1.74 FID at 1.55B after only 5 training epochs. It also achieves 30.25 dB reconstruction PSNR with an 89M decoder. After a short native-matching adaptation phase, RevFlow-XL achieves 2.85 FID using a single sampling step in each stage. Our code repository is at this https URL

33. 【2611.34781】Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Brain Tumour Classification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34781

作者：Prisha Gupta,Pradeep Kumar Gupta

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：magnetic resonance imaging, Block Attention Module, published deep learning, deep learning studies, Convolutional Block Attention

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate and early classification of brain tumours from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for treatment planning, yet many published deep learning studies are trained and tested on small, single-source datasets, reporting accuracies that may not hold outside these datasets. This study compares six deep learning architectures for four-class classification (healthy, meningioma, glioma and pituitary tumour): a baseline convolutional neural network (CNN), an attention-based CNN, ShuffleNet, and EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50 and Xception augmented with the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM). The models were trained on a corpus assembled from four publicly available MRI datasets. Because these datasets carry no patient identifiers, a leakage-aware pipeline was developed that removes exact duplicates using MD5 hashing (550 images) and near-duplicates using perceptual hashing (8,024 images), and assigns visually similar images to the same fold during 5-fold group cross-validation. A leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) test on a fully held-out dataset was used to measure cross-dataset generalisation. Under a conventional random split, ShuffleNet achieved 97.1% accuracy with 1.2M parameters. On the held-out dataset, accuracy dropped for every model: EfficientNet-B0 generalised best (78.4%), while ShuffleNetV2 fell to 66.2%. Glioma-meningioma confusion was the dominant error mode, and Grad-CAM maps showed that predictions were driven by tumour regions. These results indicate that in-distribution accuracy alone overstates real-world performance and that leakage-aware and cross-dataset evaluation should be reported alongside it.

35. 【2611.34739】Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739

作者：Ajmain Istiak Apon,Md Mehedi Hasan,Sejuti Rahman

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

备注： 26 pages, 8 figures, 10 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730

作者：Ahmed Hatem,Caoliwen Wang,Robert Hoang,Siyuan Chen,Spencer Szabados,Zike Wu,Xiyang Tan,Shenyifan Lu,Rafael Bischof,Bernd Bickel,Renjie Liao,Peter Yichen Chen

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

Abstract:Physics-derived simulation can supplement scarce real-world LiDAR intensity measurements, but its effectiveness depends on the learning architecture and the amount of real training data available. 类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：unseen image generators, unseen image, image generators, generators has driven, driven efforts

备注： 15 pages, 6 figures, and 9 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generalization to unseen image generators has driven efforts to adapt pretrained visual representations for detection. Yet recent findings show that vision foundation models already encode transferable detection knowledge, while further adaptation can impair generalization. This motivates a shift from backbone adaptation to knowledge readout. Conventional linear probing offers a direct way to read this knowledge but is limited to final-layer RGB features. Multi-cue detectors broaden the evidence base, but their designs introduce other tradeoffs: adaptation may compromise pretrained transferability, heterogeneous encoders complicate feature integration, and late fusion precludes intermediate cross-view interaction. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers-inspired framework that reads complementary knowledge from spatially aligned RGB and wavelet views within a fully frozen visual foundation. ReMoT employs staged joint attention to enable controlled local and global cross-view interaction, while learnable fusion queries progressively read complementary evidence across Transformer depth without writeback. ReMoT achieves 91.2% average accuracy across eight benchmark groups with 3,074 trainable parameters, attaining the highest overall average among the evaluated methods. These results support structured knowledge readout as a promising alternative to backbone adaptation. Code is available at this https URL.

39. 【2611.34702】Effect of class proportion shifts on classifier curvature

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34702

作者：Maxim Bolshim(1),Alexander Kugaevskikh(1) ((1) ITMO University, Saint Petersburg, Russia)

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：changing the classifier, mix can alter, Changing, target class share, target class

备注： 8 pages, 2 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Changing the class mix can alter mean loss without changing the classifier. To distinguish this reweighting from changes in the model itself, we studied ten-class CIFAR-10 classification in separate branches for two target classes, measuring the trace of the loss Hessian with respect to the output-layer parameters on fixed images of each target class. Across ten new initialisations, after 60 updates to the output layer with frozen features, the trace changed by -0.152% on average in branches where the target class share rose from 10% to 19%, and by +0.071% in branches where it fell to 1%, relative to each branch's initial trace. These changes do not isolate the effect of class share from that of the assigned training images. We developed a forecast based on initial class gradients after examining the first experiment's results and then tested it prospectively without modification. For direct increases from 10% to 19%, rather than increases after a deficit, its mean absolute error was 0.022 percentage points on the initial-trace scale, compared with 0.152 for a zero-change forecast and 0.104 for equally weighted gradients. Using the assigned class proportions improved the forecast of how much the trace would change.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Remote sensing scene understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret queried targets in relation to nearby objects and surrounding structures, which serve as spatial references or define the scope of an answer. However, standard answers and bounding boxes often leave these contextual relationships implicit. Thus, we construct GeoContext-5K to make them explicit in 5,394 training pairs for visual question answering (VQA), with spatial cues, visual facts, and supporting regions. 41. 【2611.34693】ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693

作者：Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Mike Zheng Shou

备注： 23 pages, including appendix. Video examples provided as ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: this https URL.

42. 【2611.34681】Find the Clue, Not the Scene: Rethinking Reasoning for Entity Recognition in Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34681

作者：Kyungmin Min,Soyeon Kim,Junseok Kim,Kyomin Jung

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：requires first recognizing, visual question requires, visual question answering, entity, Recognition

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Answering a knowledge-based visual question requires first recognizing the entity in the image and then recalling knowledge about it. Thinking vision-language models (VLMs) reason for thousands of tokens before answering, yet it remains underexplored how well such thinking VLMs perform each of these two steps. We therefore analyze thinking VLMs on this task and make two key observations. (1) Recognition is the bottleneck, since 76.3% of errors across four models on ViQuAE stem from misrecognizing the entity, and 84.9% of these failures are resolved once the entity name is provided in the question. (2) Recognition is driven by entity-recall clues rather than by general descriptions of the image. An entity-recall clue is a distinctive visual detail that helps the model recall the entity, such as keys, a traditional attribute of Saint Peter. Based on these findings, we propose ClueRL, a reinforcement learning approach that extends Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an advantage localized to the entity-recall clue in the thinking trace. Rather than injecting new entity knowledge, ClueRL changes how VLMs think: it leads them to attend more closely to visual details and spot these clues, helping them use the knowledge they already have. Consequently, it outperforms both GRPO and a perception-reward baseline on three entity recognition benchmarks. Moreover, this clue-seeking behavior generalizes beyond entity recognition, the only task used for training. Since recognition is the bottleneck, the gains carry over to knowledge-based visual question answering. They even extend to general multimodal reasoning, raising Qwen3.5-4B by 4.4 and 4.0 points on the ScienceQA and MMMU subsets of MMStar, respectively.

43. 【2611.34680】Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680

作者：Yuxia Zhu,Xinglong Xu,Hao Liang,Linzhuang Sun,Caijun Jia,Qifeng Cai,Jincen Luo,Lei Li,Alden Cai,Asher Qin,Wentao Zhang,Cheng Tan

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. 44. 【2611.34675】RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34675

作者：Chenzhi Liu,Jiehong Lin,Wei Huang,Xuzhao Huang,Haoyuan Li,Yang Zhang,Junchao Huang,Chenyeyang Cai,Bo Wang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://kaiknower.github.io/RoboEvo2/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Robots should adapt to unfamiliar environments and acquire new skills through interaction, then execute those skills efficiently with practice. Vision-language-action policies provide efficient control, but often need additional human demonstrations to handle unfamiliar settings; reasoning agents can discover solutions using broad prior knowledge and robot-control APIs, but repeated deliberation and repair are costly. Inspired by human learning through reasoning and practice, we propose RoboEvo$^2$, a framework for recursive harness-policy co-evolution. 45. 【2611.34657】Counting Beyond Frames: Codec Motion Tells Frozen Diffusion VLMs How Many

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657

作者：Zhuoqin Li,Jiayu Ding,Ge Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：simplest questions, hardest for video-language, diffusion alike, video-language models, count

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model's own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

47. 【2611.34645】A Hard Background Pairing Transfers across Classifiers and Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645

作者：Karl Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：images named correctly, images named, named correctly, pairing, hardest pairing

备注： 41 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:An image classifier names the object in an image, and its accuracy is the share of images named correctly. A classifier that names a car on a road may miss the same car pasted onto clouds. Composite benchmarks measure such failures by pasting objects cut from photographs onto backgrounds from other photographs. A pairing puts every object label on a different background label, such as truck on sea. The pairing on which some classifiers do worst, their hardest pairing, makes a strict test, but may be hard only for them or only on the photographs used to choose it. We show that the hardest pairing of some classifiers stays hard for others and on new photographs. We split 40 ImageNet classifiers into halves balanced by reported ImageNet accuracy. One half picks its hardest pairing of 8 object and 8 background labels from Common Objects in Context (COCO). The other half, held out of the choice, compares that pairing with random pairings of the same objects and backgrounds. For the 14 held-out classifiers with little net accuracy change after pasting objects back onto their own backgrounds, mean accuracy on composites from new photographs falls from 50.3% over random pairings to 43.7% on the hardest pairing, 6.6 percentage points lower. A benchmark using these composites can compare this pool's held-out classifiers on the fixed and random pairings.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

50. 【2611.34637】Carrying Feature Ablations Across Sparse Autoencoder Seeds

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34637

作者：Karl Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：vision encoder turns, SAE, layer, encoder, vision encoder

备注： 44 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A vision encoder turns an image into one vector per patch, refined layer by layer. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) writes each vector of one layer as a sum of learned directions, most with a coefficient of zero. Researchers call each direction a feature and edit the encoder by removing one feature. An SAE trained from another random seed learns different features. Earlier work matches the two SAEs' features one to one by how closely their directions agree. Matched features can point alike yet respond to different image parts, so removing them may change the encoder differently. We remove a feature and, separately, its match, then compare the changes in the encoder's output on the same held out images. When the second change nearly reproduces the first, we say the removal carries across seeds. A setting fixes a DINOv2 or SigLIP layer and its SAE feature count. At our tolerance, the removal carries for 12% to 70% of matched pairs across three settings. The share is 0% in two controls: an SAE whose directions stay random and, post hoc, matches shuffled among trained features. Post hoc, predicting which removals carry is more accurate with layer contributions than with directions. Some incorrect predictions remain. One persists on independent images. So pairs can be screened at the layer. Measuring the output detects errors this screen misses.

51. 【2611.34622】Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622

作者：Yue Zhou,Fangxu Han,Tony Danjun Wang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Camera-controlled video generation, Camera-controlled video, video generation anticipates, prior learned implicitly, large-scale data

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

52. 【2611.34614】Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614

作者：Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. Shum,Toby Breckon

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：introduce language reasoning, language decision, models introduce language, language, decision

备注： 28 pages, 9 figures, 19 tables. Includes supplementary material. Code will be released soon

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

53. 【2611.34608】DuCo: Exploiting Dual Cross-Layer Consistency for Sparse Geometric Attention

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34608

作者：Liangyu Xiang,Junyu Gao,Xiaofeng Wang,Changsheng Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：long image sequences, quadratic complexity limits, complexity limits efficient, capturing cross-view geometry, image sequences

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dense global attention is crucial for capturing cross-view geometry in visual geometry foundation models, yet its quadratic complexity limits efficient inference on long image sequences. Existing sparse attention methods primarily select entries of the global attention matrix based on attention probabilities and construct masks independently across layers, overlooking value redundancy and cross-layer information reuse. By analyzing attention distributions and clustering value representations across layers, we reveal two forms of \emph{cross-layer consistency}, characterized by highly similar attention probability distributions and localized changes in value redundancy structures. Building on these observations, we propose \textbf{DuCo}, a training-free sparse attention framework that exploits historical attention information and value redundancy for efficient geometric inference. Specifically, DuCo leverages historical attention information to estimate current-layer dense attention probabilities and introduces value clustering to exploit redundancy, with clusters efficiently refined through local updates across layers. Both factors are integrated through probability-weighted value distortion to jointly optimize four cluster representation states under an attention budget for sparse mask construction. Experiments across three visual geometry foundation models and five benchmarks demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-offs.

54. 【2611.34603】VisualMontage: Manipulating the Beliefs of Vision-Language Agents with Authentic Image Sequences

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34603

作者：Jinwei Hu,Lai Wei,Zhuoyun Li,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Social and Information Networks (cs.SI)

备注： Under Review

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As agents based on vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly interpret social-media content, errors in their analysis may lend credibility to rumors and amplify their spread. In this paper, we study the problem of visual cognitive collusion, in which coordinated posts combine authentic images and locally faithful captions to induce VLM agents to infer and accept attacker-chosen target claims through their own reasoning. To enable systematic evaluation of this cognitive threat, we introduce a new benchmark comprising 544 target cases and 33.2K candidate images across eight application domains. We further propose VisualMontage, a multi-agent framework for simulating such attacks through narrative planning, image selection and ordering, and caption assignment under iterative review. We evaluate this threat through extensive experiments across 12 vision-language models, examining modality effects, production-team components, and downstream defenses. Using identical attack feeds across models without victim-specific optimization, we demonstrate cross-model transferability, achieving mean attack success rates of 61.93% for proprietary models and 68.71% for open-weight models. Moreover, 92.65% of generated feeds pass independent model-based validity auditing. Even after downstream review, residual attack success rates range from 35.3% to 76.7% across the evaluated configurations. Our additional controlled comparisons further reveal that captions can improve model-rated posting naturalness while reducing attack success. Together, these findings reveal a novel cognitive threat in social-media interpretation and motivate safeguards that assess how authentic posts jointly shape agents' reasoning and conclusions.
