116. 【2611.34153】HSTTA: Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34153

作者：Hongzhou Chen,Yuhao Wei,Lianghua He,Yihang Liu,Zengqiang Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：substantial performance degradation, subjects due, retrieval suffers, suffers from substantial, substantial performance

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval suffers from substantial performance degradation on new subjects due to subject shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this shift by adapting pretrained models with unlabeled target-subject EEG. However, most existing TTA methods struggle to match supervision granularity to each query's prediction confidence, leaving many queries without reliable pseudo-labels. To address this, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation (HSTTA). HSTTA builds a hierarchy over gallery image embeddings, uses hierarchical selective prediction to assign each EEG query a supervision granularity matching its current prediction confidence, and constructs adaptation objectives accordingly. We further propose Cross-Session Consistency Calibration (CSCC), an unsupervised method that calibrates the global confidence threshold to target a predefined accuracy level for hierarchical predictions. Moreover, we introduce Reciprocal Hierarchical Backoff (RHB), which moves queries lacking reverse-matching support to coarser nodes to mitigate error reinforcement. Experiments on two public benchmarks and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of HSTTA and its key components. On THINGS-EEG2, HSTTA outperforms the strongest EEG-specific TTA baseline by 17.9 (13.4) and 8.9 (3.7) percentage points in Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, respectively, under cosine (CSLS) ranking. Further analyses show that HSTTA yields more accurate pseudo-labels during adaptation, supporting more reliable model updates.

117. 【2611.34152】Lost in the Grid: Where Multimodal LLMs Fail Between Seeing and Planning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34152

作者：Luoxi Zhang,Tianyi Tan,Jiawei Liu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Multimodal large language, Multimodal large, static visual understanding, large language models, excel at static

备注： 31 pages, 32 figures, 29 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/suat-llm/GridProbe)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at static visual understanding and are increasingly applied to spatial planning, where a model interprets a visual layout and outputs an action sequence to reach a goal. Grid games are a common testbed for this ability because their rules are explicit and a solver can verify every move. Yet existing benchmarks mostly report end-to-end success rates, which hide whether a failure stems from visual perception, rule application, or multi-step planning. We introduce GridProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that 1) locates the failing stage between visual perception and planning and 2) verifies the diagnosis by targeted fine-tuning. On Sokoban, GridProbe pairs end-to-end solving with four probes (recognition, localization, single-step prediction, and state tracking) and gives every board both as an image and as an equivalent text grid. Evaluating seven MLLMs on Sokoban and a maze game reveals distinct bottlenecks behind similar success rates: proprietary models are limited by multi-step planning, whereas open-source models are limited by rule application or by visual grid indexing, i.e., counting rows and columns. Planning failures also hide perception failures: when planning is easier, the visual cost of the same model grows from 7.6 to 37.7 points. The diagnosis identifies what to train: fine-tuning only the vision module of the perception-limited model on board reading raises its interactive Sokoban success 3.5-fold (5.8% to 20.4%) and transfers zero-shot to the unseen maze game (single-turn success 13.8% to 67.1%), whereas the same data does not help a rule-limited model and training on solver solutions brings no gain. We will release the benchmark and code at this https URL.

118. 【2611.34143】Non-Linear Motion from Two Frames: Cubic Hermite Trajectories for Video Frame Interpolation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34143

作者：Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Mohammad Ali Moni

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Two-frame video frame, Two-frame video, constant-velocity straight-line path, commonly resamples endpoint, resamples endpoint optical

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Two-frame video frame interpolation commonly resamples endpoint optical flow by the query time, which selects a constant-velocity straight-line path even though the same endpoint displacement is compatible with many intermediate trajectories. We present HermiteVFI, which replaces independent query-time motion prediction with one explicit continuous trajectory per pixel. The measured endpoint displacement fixes the two trajectory positions, while two latent endpoint derivatives learned from the frame pair control the interior path. A cubic Hermite curve is the minimum-degree polynomial that can independently satisfy these two position and two derivative boundary quantities. Its residual form contains linear motion exactly, structurally preserves the measured displacement endpoints for any network output, and has coefficients that are predicted once per frame pair and evaluated in closed form at arbitrary t. We further show that one interior query time gives a rank-deficient constraint on the two free trajectory coefficients, whereas any two distinct interior times are full rank at the representation level, motivating multi-time supervision. Appearance-derived directional cues help infer the latent derivatives that endpoint displacement alone cannot identify. On X4K1000FPS, SNU-FILM, and Vimeo90K-Septuplet, the largest gains occur for difficult non-uniform motion and high interpolation ratios. The amortization applies to trajectory-coefficient prediction; downstream reversal, refinement, and synthesis remain query dependent.

119. 【2611.34141】Spe3DUp: Faithful Trajectory Distillation for Fast Image-to-3D Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34141

作者：Liangchao Xu,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Yao Yao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：produce high-quality meshes, fine geometric details, applications in games, digital design, meshes with complex

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent 3D generative models can produce high-quality meshes with complex structures and fine geometric details, with broad applications in games, film, and digital design. However, high-quality generation typically relies on long multi-step sampling processes that repeatedly evaluate large networks, leading to long inference times. At the same time, reference-driven tasks such as game-asset reconstruction and product digitization demand close correspondence to the input image: generated geometry must faithfully reconstruct the specific reference object. Acceleration must therefore reduce sampling computation while preserving the model's ability to reconstruct that object. To enable fast and faithful 3D generation, we introduce Spe3DUp, a coarse-to-fine framework based on Faithful Trajectory Distillation (FTD). FTD directly supervises few-step students with the teacher's input-conditioned denoising trajectories to preserve its generation behavior. We apply FTD separately to coarse geometry generation and geometric refinement, compressing both sampling processes while maintaining correspondence to the reference input. We further accelerate the complete mesh-generation pipeline with efficient geometry decoding and parallel remeshing. Experiments show that Spe3DUp maintains geometric quality and input correspondence close to those of the teacher, accelerates the coarse and refinement DiTs by 10.0x and 19.9x, respectively, and achieves a 7.7x end-to-end speedup over the original teacher pipeline.

120. 【2611.34140】Benefit of Structured Representations: When Equivariance Helps in Image Classification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34140

作者：Florian A. Hölzl,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：neural networks promise, networks promise improved, Equivariant neural networks, promise improved representation, improved representation learning

备注： NeurIPS 2026 Workshop NeurReps Proceedings

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Equivariant neural networks promise improved representation learning through structural guarantees. However, mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries. Images are a particularly interesting modality with many such symmetries and extensive datasets from which to learn this structure. In this work, we investigate when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases. We study the discrete rotation group of quarter-turns (C4) which acts losslessly without interpolation or boundary artifacts. Combined with image classification as a controlled invariant objective, this allows for a principled empirical investigation into the role of structural guarantees. We introduce ConvNeXt-C4 as a rotation equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations. Training from scratch, our experiments show, that the C4 variant outperforms its base model across all tasks while operating with 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs. Its performance advantage increases log-linearly with decreasing training set size, with pre-training providing a similar improvement across both model variants. Analyzing the classifier head reveals that orientation information from the representations' structure is actively used, improving performance by 4.90% across all datasets. Our results highlight the sample-efficiency benefits of equivariance and the value of retained orientation information, motivating further investigation beyond image classification. Code is available at this https URL.

121. 【2611.34139】An Image Detectability Field as a Viewpoint Utility for 2D-3D Localization in Sparse Maps

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34139

作者：Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：field, image, planner, image detectability field, point

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In 2D-3D localization, a camera estimates its pose by matching an image to a 3D map. A camera held in a hand or mounted on a robot can often choose its viewpoint before it takes the image. In this work, we ask whether an image detectability field is a useful viewpoint utility when the planner reads only the geometry of the map. The field can be queried at any 3D point near a surface. From the local geometry around the point, it predicts how likely a 2D image detector is to fire at the projection of the point. We use the trained field as it is, without fine-tuning it for view selection. A fixed SuperPoint and EPnP-RANSAC localizer then estimates the pose from the chosen view. We answer in two settings, each with a test registered before the runs. On synthetic ShapeNet objects with sparse point maps, summing the field over the visible landmarks beats counting them by +2.0 points of localization success rate (object-clustered 95% interval [+0.7, +3.2]). It also beats summing Harris-3D ranks by +2.2 [+0.8, +3.5]. On real indoor scenes from SceneFun3D, our adaptation of the learned view scorer LWL falls below counting on the test rooms. We therefore add the field to a learned per-landmark planner. On the test rooms with sparse maps whose landmarks the field also selects, the planner with the field beats the same planner without it by +1.8 points. It beats the planner with Harris-3D or curvature in its place by +1.0 and +0.8 points. All three room-clustered intervals lie above zero. In both settings, the field helps in sparse maps and is on par with Harris-3D in dense maps.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reference banks used for industrial image inspection can be compressed to fit a deployment budget, but the resulting anomaly decisions depend on both the compressed scores and the operating threshold. 作者：Shenxing Wei,Jinxi Li,Zihui Zhang,Ajay Kumar,Wei Wang,Bo Yang

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate 3D surface reconstruction from unposed images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Recent feed-forward pointmap methods eliminate the need for camera poses but inherently produce discrete, sparse representations. In this paper, we present a generalizable pipeline that directly recovers continuous, high-fidelity 3D surfaces from unposed images by seamlessly integrating pointmap- and ray-based representations. 124. 【2611.34126】Out of Sight, Not Out of Clues: Can Video LLMs Guess from Partial Evidence?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34126

作者：Luoxi Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Yanlin Luo,Peiyang He,Junlin Zhu,Chun To Choi,Yang Xiong,Jiang Wu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large Language Models, Video Large Language, Large Language, Language Models, Video Large

备注： 27 pages, 21 figures, 23 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In real-world applications like egocentric recording, visual accessibility, and surveillance review, visual evidence for Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) is often only partially captured. A reliable model should match its response to the evidence: answering when sufficient, guessing from visible clues when partial, and abstaining when absent. Yet existing benchmarks enforce a binary answerable-or-unanswerable split, leaving partial evidence unmeasured and obscuring whether failures arise from missing evidence or misjudging sufficiency. We introduce ClueProbe, a diagnostic benchmark to (1) separate perceiving evidence from judging sufficiency, and (2) validate our diagnosis via targeted fine-tuning. ClueProbe comprises 1,721 human-annotated questions across 598 natural videos, labeled Answerable, Guessable, or Unanswerable, alongside controlled perturbations altering wording or visual input. Evaluating 10 open-source and proprietary Video LLMs reveals that failures stem from judgment rather than perception: models perceive the evidence yet lack graded sufficiency awareness, answering or refusing outright while rarely guessing from partial clues. Targeted fine-tuning shows that this reflects missing supervision rather than insufficient capacity: supervising the guess level lifts appropriate guessing from 1.6% to 40.4% without extra false refusals, whereas conventional refusal tuning rejects 92% of guessable questions. Data and code will be released at this https URL.

126. 【2611.34108】Detectable by Design: Learning a Continuous 3D Saliency Field from 2D Detector Responses

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34108

作者：Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：point, points, choice matters, map decide, point cloud

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The 3D points kept in a map decide whether a camera can be localized against it, and this choice matters more as the map becomes sparser. Existing 3D keypoint detectors usually define saliency by semantics, repeatability, reconstruction or descriptor matchability. None of these definitions asks whether a point will actually be detected in an image, and as a result most of the points they select are not detected again in images. In this paper, we define 3D saliency as image detectability: the response of a 2D interest-point detector at a surface point, aggregated over all views in which the point is visible. We propose DetField, a continuous field over 3D space that takes a point cloud as input and predicts detectability at any location around it. The supervision is the response of a 2D detector, back-projected from images onto the point cloud. Multi-scale curvature invariants are passed to the decoder at every query point, bypassing the voxel pooling of the encoder. In a localization benchmark that isolates the choice of 3D points, DetField outperforms Harris-3D and five learned 3D keypoint methods on objects and on real indoor scenes. On unseen rooms, it improves localization success by 7.4 points over Harris-3D, the strongest baseline. On objects, it also leads under a 2D detector not used in training. On sparse maps, it stays ahead of Harris-3D on both objects and real scenes.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Humans can recognize novel concepts from only a few observations by identifying defining visual attributes and eliminating alternative interpretations. However, a single observation is consistent with multiple possible concepts, and accurate visual perception alone is insufficient to determine the intended interpretation. Negative evidence can reduce this ambiguity by eliminating competing hypotheses. Inspired by Quine's gavagai problem, we introduce the Gavagai Benchmark to evaluate whether vision-language models can identify the attributes defining a novel concept from positive and negative examples. Abstract:Cross-modal homography estimation requires reliable correspondences despite appearance differences between sensing modalities. Multi-scale refinement progressively reduces the search range, but its accuracy remains limited by the ability of matching features to distinguish nearby candidates. Abstract:In this paper, we introduce EiT by exploring the key design factors of pixel-space diffusion from three perspectives: model architecture, representation, and frequency domain. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34084

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Lightweight trackers have replaced cross-correlation with attention, and on low-power edge hardware they pay for it, since memory-bound softmax, normalization and reshaping operators of attention cost far more time than their arithmetic suggests. Benchmarking efficient trackers on an edge Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and an Arm CPU under one numerically validated protocol, we find that correlation trackers are the fast ones, but they lag in accuracy unless they adopt a ViT backbone, which brings attention back. We revisit what correlation lacks. Template--search interaction serves two roles: conditioning the search features on the target and matching them against it spatially. A descriptor of the target scales and shifts the search features channel by channel inside the backbone, and kernels aligned with the target box, each with a weight predicted from the template, correlate with them in one matrix product. With the backbone fixed, our correlation remains essential while incurring negligible NPU overhead, offering a better speed-accuracy trade-off than the attention-based interactions we tested. TaCo-T achieves 62.3% LaSOT AUC at 184 FPS on the Rockchip RV1126B NPU and 96 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU, 3.0x and 3.5x the NPU speed of HiT-Tiny and AsymTrack-T at higher accuracy. 135. 【2611.34078】Projector Subspace Optimization for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34078

作者：Hao Yu,Quan Cheng,Lijun Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Large Language Models, Multimodal Large Language, instruction tuning, Continual Instruction Tuning, Multimodal Continual Instruction

备注： 29 pages, 12 figures, including supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, yet adapting them to sequential tasks requires Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). Existing replay-free methods expand prompts or routed LoRA experts across multi-layer decoders, incurring heavy parameter and routing overhead while shifting internal feature distributions. Instead, we identify the final linear layer of the vision-language projector as a high-capacity, coordinate-stationary bridge, where sequential updates easily induce interface drift by displacing historical visual tokens. While low-rank subspace adaptation offers a compact remedy, selecting directions solely from historical statistics or task gradients sacrifices plasticity or stability, and unconstrained multi-step updates still accumulate large drift. To resolve both issues, we propose Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions (PROUD), a continual subspace optimization framework for the terminal projector layer. At task onset, PROUD combines retained input statistics with initial gradients to analytically extract a metric-orthonormal subspace maximizing first-order task gain under a drift budget. During training, it regularizes updates within this fixed subspace via an exact Frobenius penalty before losslessly merging them with zero inference or routing overhead. Experiments on UCIT and CoIN across MLLM backbones show that PROUD outperforms recent replay-free baselines in continual accuracy while curbing forgetting with a fraction of trainable parameters.

136. 【2611.34075】Hijacking 3D Generation: Targeted Adversarial Attacks against Image-to-3D Models via Prefix Trajectory Alignment

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34075

作者：Yilong Yang,Jiale Teng,Yule Liu,Yang Liu,Xinlei He,Zhuo Ma

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：models enable users, generate high-quality, enable users, users to generate, Online services

备注： 18 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Online services that deploy Image-to-3D models enable users to generate high-quality 3D assets from a single image. However, the generated 3D geometry can violate content policies and cause broader security risks. To prevent such content, these services typically use policy-restricted inspectors to examine uploaded images before generation. Since the inspector examines only the visible content of the input, a visually benign image can still induce the generation of policy-restricted 3D geometry. However, turning this vulnerability into an effective attack is non-trivial in the black-box setting: global backpropagation through the long DiT generation trajectory is difficult to fit, while different image preprocessing pipelines across services can substantially reduce transferability. In this paper, we present PTA3D, a targeted adversarial attack against Image-to-3D models that addresses these challenges through Prefix Trajectory Alignment (PTA) and Preprocessing Aware Optimization (PAO). PTA progressively aligns the prefix DiT outputs of the adversarial trajectory with those of the target trajectory, where the core geometric structure is formed, allowing the final geometry to be controlled without optimizing through the complete generation trajectory. PAO exposes adversarial optimization to diverse image transformations rather than a single preprocessing pipeline, reducing sensitivity to the mismatch between surrogate and target services. We establish a prefix-to-output bound and a preprocessing-aware transfer bound to theoretically characterize the effectiveness of the two designs. Experiments on three open-source Image-to-3D models and three online services show that PTA3D achieves 41.5%-88.5% ASR on online services and 90.8%-94.6% ASR in white box settings, substantially outperforming existing attacks.

137. 【2611.34067】When to Respond, Where to Ground: Streaming 3D Affordance Grounding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34067

作者：Wenting Luan,Weilong Yan,Siqi Lu,Ming Li,Beibei Lin,Yongbin Zheng,Robby T. Tan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：continuous RGB-D stream, affordance grounding requires, affordance grounding methods, continuous RGB-D, affordance grounding

备注： 19 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model's own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

138. 【2611.34064】SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34064

作者：Zesong Yang,Weikai Chen,Liyuan Cui,Yuan Li,Jiaxin Huang,Lutao Jiang,Runze Zhang,Yingda Yin,Xiaoyang Huang,Kai Yan,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. 139. 【2611.34062】Surf-Style: Surface-Based Scene Stylization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34062

作者：Wanrong Hong,Ancheng Lin,Mukesh Prasad,Jun Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：reality content creation, virtual reality content, visual computing applications, artistic scene rendering, immersive visualization

备注： 10 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Consistent and controllable 3D stylization is critical for visual computing applications such as immersive visualization, augmented and virtual reality content creation, and artistic scene rendering. While 2D image stylization has advanced significantly, extending these methods to 3D remains challenging due to the need for cross-view consistency, where stylized renderings must align across all viewpoints. Applying 2D stylization independently to each rendered view often results in inconsistencies across perspectives. Existing methods attempt to improve consistency by leveraging implicit radiance field representations, which model scene appearance as a function of spatial locations. However, these methods struggle with ambiguity in updating colors, as the indirect dependence between pixel appearance and learnable scene parameters makes it difficult to enforce consistent stylization during optimization. This work presents Surface-based Scene Stylization (Surf-Style), which reconstructs object surfaces in a 3D scene and uses these surfaces to achieve geometric consistency and localized stylization. Surf-Style defines a learnable appearance model over 3D space, which is modified to replicate the visual style of a reference image. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior consistency and offers greater flexibility in controllable stylization, including the ability to target specific surface regions and combine different styles.

140. 【2611.34056】Understanding Diffusion Acceleration: Methods, Trade-Offs, and Open Questions

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34056

作者：Xiaofeng Mao,Fu-yun Wang,Le Zhuo,Zhengkai Jiang,Shaohao Rui,Weijie Ma

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：flow-based generative models, generative models support, models support high-quality, Diffusion and flow-based, support high-quality image

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion and flow-based generative models support high-quality image, audio, and video synthesis. Sampling can require dozens to hundreds of denoiser evaluations, with each pass becoming more expensive as resolution, video length, or model size increases. This cost affects latency and energy use in interactive and edge applications. We examine four approaches to accelerating inference: distillation, consistency training, sampling methods, and denoiser design. We express training objectives, transition maps, and numerical updates in a common notation to compare changes to the learning target, the sampling trajectory, and the work performed within each denoiser pass. Published image and video benchmarks illustrate the quality--cost trade-offs of one- and few-step generation, the interaction between solvers and timestep schedules, the effect of feature caching, and the difference between first-frame latency and sustained streaming throughput. We close with open problems in visual-native architectures and post-compression distillation, solver-aware few-step training, and semantic trajectory alignment with factorized distribution matching.

141. 【2611.34046】End-to-End Image-Goal Navigation for Agile Quadrotors via Recurrent State Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34046

作者：Simone Nascivera,Ismail Geles,Davide Scaramuzza

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：reach the viewpoint, target photograph, goal pose, goal, unseen environments

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image-goal navigation asks a robot to reach the viewpoint of a target photograph without a map or goal pose. In three dimensions, this requires not only recognizing the goal but also estimating 3D displacement and generating continuous control actions to approach and settle at the target view. This is difficult because the same visual difference between current and goal image can require very different actions depending on the robot's velocity, yet monocular images alone reveal neither metric scale nor body motion. As a result, existing image-goal policies are limited to planar action spaces and rely on a state estimator for flight control. Policies that map directly from images to low-level control commands have been demonstrated for agile flight, but not toward goal images in unseen environments. We present the first image-goal navigation policy that directly commands thrust and angular rates for agile quadrotor flight in unseen 3D environments, without relying on state estimation (e.g., SLAM, VO, or SfM) or a separate velocity controller. We train a recurrent vision-based policy through a four-stage pipeline that combines geometric and motion supervision with privileged-teacher distillation and end-to-end reinforcement learning fine-tuning. On 1000 held-out flights in unseen environments, the policy achieves an 82.3% success rate with a mean terminal position error of 0.10m across successful flights. Deployed zero-shot on a real quadrotor, the policy reaches an 84% success rate over 25 trials in an unseen indoor environment.

142. 【2611.34032】Counter-DECEIVR: Counterfactual Explanations for Deepfake Detectors using Detector-Guided Rectified Flow Inversion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34032

作者：Mariano V. Ntrougkas,Vasileios Mezaris,Ioannis Patras

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：media are increasingly, driving an arms, increasingly passed, arms race, race between image

备注： Under review

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep-learning-generated media are increasingly passed off as real, driving an arms race between image generators and deepfake detectors. Trust in the latter is limited by their often black-box nature; and, while region-based explanations can sometimes be extracted, these become less useful as generation quality improves. Counterfactual examples, which show the minimal change that flips a detector's decision, reveal what evidence drives a verdict, helping analysts decide whether to trust it. This work introduces Counter-DECEIVR (DEtector-guided reCtifiEd flow InVeRsion for counterfactuals), a novel training-free framework that generates counterfactual examples. Counter-DECEIVR steers latents in the velocity field of a rectified flow model using gradients from off-the-shelf deepfake detectors, without training a generator or adapting the detector; unlike prior methods, it requires no per-instance or per-detector fitting and no diffusion training on the target data. It also leverages the detector's explanation maps to concentrate editing to salient regions, turning low-level feature importance into localised counterfactual edits. A single parameter controls the strength of the edits. Across two deepfake detectors and two widely used forensics datasets, Counter-DECEIVR outperforms prior counterfactual generation methods, including training-based ones. Code will be made available upon acceptance.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34027

备注： 11 pages, 3 figures. Main paper only

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Detailed hand motion contributes to the realism and expressiveness of generated human motion. Completing these details from body motion and language requires connecting the context of an action to the articulation of the hands. We introduce ContextHands, which completes hand motion from an observed body sequence and text descriptions assigned to hands and time intervals. Abstract:Due to the Deep Learning requirement for a large training dataset, Transfer Learning has become a central method in the field of Computer Vision, which heavily used Deep Learning. Since the adoption of transfer learning in the field, the performance of the models in computer vision is significantly upgraded. The common transfer learning practice in computer vision research is to use state-of-the-art architectures in the ImageNet dataset as the backbone network. The best performing architecture in ImageNet is currently held by EfficientNet, which is a set of architectures that were progressively grown from a baseline architecture. Abstract:Foundation models (FMs) have advanced visual understanding and generation by providing semantically rich representations. However, this role of FMs remains underexplored in human motion. Large motion datasets offer diverse movements at scale, but often lack action labels for systematically evaluating learned representations on understanding tasks. Skeleton datasets enable such evaluation through established recognition protocols, yet are typically smaller and focus on predefined actions captured in controlled environments. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Unsupervised cross-modal homography estimation is challenging because appearance differences obscure shared geometric structures. Separating modality transfer from homography estimation helps reduce these differences, but accurate alignment still requires effective geometric representations and consistency constraints on the two subtasks. We propose GeoCo (Geometry-Aware and Consistency-Constrained Homography Estimation), a framework that addresses these two requirements without cross-modal transformation labels. An intermediate model first learns to perform cross-modal matching without modality transfer, then transfers prediction and feature knowledge to the student, eliminating online VFM extraction. GeoCo-D also outperforms all compared baselines while using 98.5% fewer parameters and 70.4% fewer FLOPs than the complete GeoCo teacher.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World-space human mesh recovery can lose spatial consistency even when camera-space body estimates are accurate. A single sequence-wide scale resolves global scale ambiguity but cannot correct local scale distortion along a reconstructed camera path. We present ScaleCurve, a framework that estimates time-varying metric corrections from human-ground geometry. We construct scale observations by comparing predicted foot geometry with locally fitted ground planes. Joint temporal optimization downweights inconsistent observations and couples neighboring scale estimates, allowing calibration to adapt along the camera path without freely following measurement noise. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spatial intelligence concerns both scene structure and its temporal evolution. Yet a semantically correct description can conceal a quantitative error: recognizing that objects are approaching does not determine their closing speed or whether they will meet. Such errors are critical in collision avoidance, trajectory tracking, and reactive grasping, where actions depend on motion magnitude, timing, and relative geometry. We study quantitative kinematic understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): resolving motion magnitude, rate, reference-frame dependence, and future spatial evolution from visual evidence. We introduce the Quantitative Kinematics Benchmark (QK-Bench), comprising 2,341 multiple-choice QA pairs from 379 videos and 17 tasks spanning Object-Centric Kinematics, Camera Ego-Motion Kinematics, and Multi-Object Kinematic Relations. Hidden geometric and temporal signals support trajectory construction and filtering, while fixed kinematic rules map physical quantities to verifiable answers, including categorical labels. Trajectory and question validation ensure target reliability and sufficient visual support in the sampled frames. All models receive a unified monocular RGB input. Evaluations of proprietary, open-source, and spatial MLLMs reveal substantial limitations: the strongest model achieves only 41.2\% overall accuracy, and spatial MLLMs show no consistent advantage. These findings indicate that current advances in spatial intelligence do not reliably transfer to quantitative kinematic understanding.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33990

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Geospatial foundation model (GFM) research papers are plagued by inconsistent evaluation settings (e.g., different pre-processing and probes), making it difficult to separate performance from evaluation choices. We propose geo-eval-harness}, an open-source framework for controlled model comparisons. We use this harness to evaluate 100 GFMs and general vision models on 6 classification and 14 segmentation datasets, using multiple probes on frozen or low-rank-adapted encoders. Our geo-eval-harness makes controlled experiments easy, fast, and fully reproducible. We release the code, configurations, and results at this https URL.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Departing from the paradigm of diffusion and autoregressive models, Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) enables high-quality image generation through iterative refinements. However, this repeated processing over the entire visual representations results in substantial computation overhead. Furthermore, the binary representations and global refinement mechanism in GRN disable the direct application of acceleration strategies developed for other generative models. To accelerate GRN model, we first observe several key properties by analyzing the entire generative process. That includes the stage characteristic, dual branch similarity and more importantly, the $\textbf{refinement redundancy}$. For alleviating the refinement redundancy mainly resulting from the detail refinement stage, we propose Adaptive Retention Expansion ($\textbf{ARE}$) and Selective Token Freezing ($\textbf{STF}$) to shrink refinement steps and reduce token computations, respectively. In the experiments on GenEval and DPG-Bench benchmarks, our method $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ achieves a $2.03\times$ sampling speedup while largely preserving image generation performance. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33964

备注： 18 pages,8+3 ref+7 sup,6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long sequence reconstruction must correct accumulated drift while preserving locally consistent geometry. In robotics and autonomous driving, embodied acquisition provides recurring platform- environment geometry and repeated observations of scene structure and platform motion. We present Embodied-VGGT, which exploits these cues to establish reliable local relationships and selectively preserve them during global refinement. Memory-Augmented Grounded Chunks (MAGC) builds on Scal3R's global context memory and uses relative scale references derived from predicted ground geometry to regularize test-time memory updates, encouraging cross-chunk consistency while limiting changes to reliable local geometry. Superpoint Alignment (SA) and Cross-Loop Optimization (CL) jointly address the robust establishment and preservation of geometric relationships. SA balances ground and non-ground evidence across spatial regions to estimate inter-chunk translation. CL uses geometric and motion consistency across interleaved windows to select coherent chunk groups, each receiving a shared Sim(3) correction that preserves its internal relative transformations during global optimization. Experiments on five public driving benchmarks demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements in long sequence reconstruction. On KITTI Odometry, Embodied-VGGT achieves a mean absolute trajectory error of 7.30 m and a Chamfer distance of 2.67 m, reducing these errors by 46.8% and 23.3% compared with the state-of-the-art baseline under same evaluation protocol. 153. 【2611.33963】argeted Adaptation of World-Action Models via Interaction and Motion Heads

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World-Action Models (WAMs) are a promising new paradigm of robot manipulation policies that jointly generate future video and actions. Successful manipulation requires both identifying where to interact and modeling how the robot and objects move. 155. 【2611.33957】RefMix: Expanding Endpoint Proposals for Diversity-Preserving Reward Fine-Tuning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33957

作者：Jaemin Kim,Christian Simon,Dongseok Shim,Wei-Yao Wang,Qiyu Wu,Masato Ishii,Shusuke Takahashi,Jong Chul Ye,Yuki Mitsufuji

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：aligns flow-based generative, matching aligns flow-based, flow-based generative models, repeatedly generating, Online forward matching

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://kjm981995.github.io/RefMix)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Online forward matching aligns flow-based generative models by repeatedly generating, rewarding, and fitting model-produced endpoints. However, this behavior creates an on-policy feedback loop: as high-reward modes become increasingly favored, alternative modes become less likely to appear in subsequent training pools, progressively reducing visual diversity. We introduce RefMix, a reference-assisted endpoint proposal strategy that addresses this limitation by injecting alternative candidates into the training pool while retaining the original task reward and forward matching objective. In particular, RefMix uses a reference model during early high-noise steps to expose alternative visual modes, followed by the current reward-tuned model for endpoint completion. Across state-of-the-art online forward matching methods, RefMix consistently improves visual diversity while preserving task reward performance. Controlled inference experiments further demonstrate that training-time endpoint exposure is critical to improve reward--diversity trade-offs, beyond the impact of reference-assisted inference. By rethinking how training endpoints are proposed, RefMix achieves up to 44% higher diversity over forward matching baselines at comparable reward levels.

156. 【2611.33948】Where to Approximate: Understanding and Accelerating Looped Multi-View Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33948

作者：Siyuan Zhang,Yuhan Chen,Yangyang Ning,Nan Wang,Jun Yan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Looped multi-view reconstruction, refines scene features, Looped multi-view, multi-view reconstruction refines, reconstruction refines scene

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Looped multi-view reconstruction refines scene features by repeatedly applying a shared transformer block. Making these updates cheaper requires knowing how an approximation at one step affects the rest of the refinement. We study this with paired interventions in Déjà View and stacked reconstruction transformers, separating the error an approximation injects, how that error propagates, and how it affects the output. In Déjà View, approximation errors are attenuated more strongly than matched random perturbations under a scale-adjusted state-error measure, and a local Jacobian decomposition separates the contribution of channel gating from the response to spatial error structure. Spatial approximations made early benefit from subsequent refinement, whereas cross-step reuse depends on which quantity is cached and how reuse steps are spaced. At equal final norm, approximation errors change the predicted rotations 1.55 times as much as random errors. These findings guide our proposed method, which reduces spatial resolution and pools keys and values early in refinement, then interleaves attention-output reuse with full attention, and is adapted through lightweight self-distillation. Across five reconstruction benchmarks, the balanced configuration runs $1.63\times$ faster than the official 16-step Déjà View model with a 1.86-point reduction in average pose AUC@$3^\circ$. At matched latency, it exceeds the interpolated curve of a trained baseline using full refinement blocks by 4.73 points in AUC@$3^\circ$ and improves the corresponding geometry metrics.

157. 【2611.33947】A Computational Model of Continuous Recognition Memory for Natural Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33947

作者：Sheng Zhao,Yijin Wang,Bei Xiao,Yuhao Zhu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：human visual memory, basic function, memory, Recognizing, CRM

备注： 18 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recognizing an image we have seen before is a basic function of human visual memory. Understanding human continuous recognition memory (CRM) can help machine vision systems predict which images people are likely to remember and generate more memorable images. It can also inform the diagnosis of memory impairments and the development of interventions to improve memory. This paper proposes the first computational model for CRM. The model captures three key components of continuous recognition memory (CRM): image encoding, memory decay, and decision making. It encodes each image as a distinct random variable in a perceptual space. As the memory of an image fades, the distribution of its random variable drifts within that space. To decide whether the current image has been seen before, the observer retrieves samples from the distributions associated with previously encoded images and compares them with the current image. Our model predicts human observers' trial-by-trial responses in CRM tasks within the variability observed across human participants. Our model is parameter efficient, requiring only four shared parameters and two parameters per observer. It can accurately predict the memorability of an image no worse than prior methods that require millions of parameters.

158. 【2611.33940】AT-Det: A Dataset for Testicular Appendage Torsion Detection and Segmentation in Ultrasound Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33940

作者：Ying Jiang,Juntao Jiang,Linxuan Fan,Lingling Li,Yaqi Wang,Xiuzhen Yang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Jingjing Ye

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Testicular appendage torsion, acute scrotal pain, Testicular appendage, acute scrotal emergencies, appendage torsion

备注： 17 pages, 9 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Background: Testicular appendage torsion (TAT) is the leading cause of acute scrotal pain in prepubertal children. Yet, its diagnosis remains challenging due to nonspecific clinical manifestations and the absence of standardized datasets for pediatric acute scrotal emergencies. Results: We present TAT-Det, an expert-annotated ultrasound dataset specifically designed for TAT. TAT-Det comprises 2,600 ultrasound images from 1,203 patients collected at the Children's Hospital, Zhejiang University School of Medicine. Each TAT-positive image is accompanied by lesion-level bounding boxes for detection and pixel-level segmentation masks for fine-grained delineation. Annotations were produced through a multi-stage workflow involving independent annotation by board-certified pediatric sonographers, consensus review, and refinement. Baseline benchmarks for object detection and semantic segmentation using representative deep learning models are further provided, with YOLO11m achieving the best detection performance and RWKV-UNet the best segmentation performance. Conclusions: TAT-Det addresses the lack of publicly available resources for pediatric emergency ultrasound and establishes a foundation for developing and evaluating artificial intelligence methods for automated TAT analysis.

159. 【2611.33935】BayesPath: Reliability-Aware Bayesian Reasoning for Whole-Slide Pathology Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33935

作者：Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Jongwoo Kim,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diagnosing whole-slide images, requires hypothesis-driven reasoning, Diagnosing whole-slide, seek discriminative evidence, whole-slide images

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diagnosing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires hypothesis-driven reasoning: pathologists form a differential diagnosis, seek discriminative evidence, weigh its reliability, and stop once it suffices. However, existing training-free WSI agents lack an explicit framework for such reasoning. They typically use a large language model (LLM) to aggregate the region descriptions of a multimodal LLM (MLLM), without a belief over the candidates or regard for the descriptions' reliability. Consequently, a repeated model error counts as independent evidence for an incorrect diagnosis. We introduce BayesPath, a training-free WSI agent that casts the pathologist's Scan-Look-Weigh-Decide process as reliability-aware Bayesian reasoning. An explicit belief over the candidates is updated with each observation through a reliability-aware likelihood and guides where to look next and when to stop. This likelihood corrects for the models' answer bias, discounts correlated observations, and captures diagnostic confusions estimated without labels. The updates form an interpretable evidence trail of how each observation shapes the belief. On five MultiPathQA tasks spanning slide classification and slide VQA, BayesPath achieves the highest average accuracy among training-free agents, 58.6% against 34.1% for the next best, with large gains on slide classification. On identical observations, Bayesian updating outperforms LLM aggregation on average, even with a larger LLM. BayesPath's belief is also better calibrated than naive Bayes, so uncertain cases can be deferred to pathologists. Code is available at this https URL

Abstract:Flow-based generative policies offer expressive action modeling for embodied control, yet achieving efficient one-step generation under reward-driven policy optimization remains challenging. 162. 【2611.33920】UPP-Gauss: Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting for B-mode Novel-View Synthesis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33920

作者：Guil Jung,Seok-Hwan Oh,Myeong-Gee Kim,Young-Min Kim,Hyeon-Jik Lee,Sang-Yun Kim,Hyeok-Jun Kwon,Hyuk-Sool Kwon,Hyeon-Min Bae

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：resulting appearance variations, Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting, Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian, Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed, ultrasound images requires

备注： 5 pages, 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate novel-view synthesis of B-mode ultrasound images requires modeling acoustic propagation through tissue and the resulting appearance variations across probe poses. We propose Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting (UPP-Gauss), which integrates these acoustic interactions into an explicit Gaussian representation. UPP-Gauss constructs acoustic maps by sampling three 3D Gaussian fields using the probe pose and acoustic beam propagation direction, then combines these maps according to ultrasound propagation to synthesize B-mode images. Across three acoustically diverse datasets, UPP-Gauss achieves the best reconstruction quality with efficient rendering, improving PSNR over the best baseline on each dataset by 1.43 dB on average. These results demonstrate accurate and efficient B-mode novel-view synthesis through explicit Gaussian modeling of ultrasound propagation.

163. 【2611.33917】Event-WAM: Learning to Act with Event-Derived Futures

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33917

作者：Jongseong Bae,Hyunwoo Kim,Yeongin Lee,Eubin So,Ha Young Kim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：World Action Models, recent studies exploring, studies exploring alternative, World Action, Action Models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World Action Models (WAMs) leverage future visual modeling for robotic action learning, with recent studies exploring alternative or complementary objectives beyond conventional RGB prediction. We introduce Event-WAM, which incorporates event-derived visual changes as auxiliary future supervision alongside RGB. Event-WAM constructs temporally structured event representations from RGB demonstrations and encodes them using a frozen pretrained RGB video VAE, without training an event-specific VAE. Joint RGB--Event latent flow matching provides appearance- and change-oriented supervision without requiring event observations or explicit future generation at inference. Across simulated benchmarks, Event-WAM maintains comparable performance under standard conditions, while robustness gains under perturbations are benchmark-dependent, including improvements on LIBERO-Plus. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate improved manipulation success under both standard and unseen perturbation conditions. Further analyses show that policy improvements are not consistently accompanied by higher future prediction fidelity, suggesting the potential of change-oriented supervision for action learning.

164. 【2611.33915】SEEK: Self-Evolution through Execution Knowledge for Agentic Robot Manipulation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33915

作者：Zeyang Guan,Qihao Zhao,Rui Gong,Zhang Haitian,Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Lingao Xiao,Haozhe Du,Xuehui Yu,Ye Lu,Jianjun Gao,Ruoyu Wang,Haowen Bai,Hao Li,Scarlett Li,Kim-Hui Yap

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：General-purpose multimodal foundation, General-purpose multimodal, multimodal foundation models, foundation models enable, demonstrations or training

备注： 17 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:General-purpose multimodal foundation models enable robots to adapt during deployment from observations and execution feedback, without task-specific demonstrations or training. However, the robot retains what it learns only in the model's working context. Once the task ends, it loses this execution knowledge, such as how to carry a bowl between two grippers. On the next attempt at the task, the robot may take just as long and repeat the same mistakes. To avoid this repetition, robots must retain execution knowledge across attempts. Yet replaying past actions is insufficient, because their effects depend on the current conditions. Procedures that determine actions from the current scene address this, but when they meet conditions that earlier attempts did not cover, revising them requires the measured effects of earlier actions. We therefore propose SEEK, an agentic framework that retains both, enabling robots to improve across attempts while keeping the underlying model fixed. To adapt retained knowledge to the current scene, SEEK adopts a Self-Evolving Programmatic Policy (SEPP) that encodes manipulation as executable procedures and determines each action from current observations. As the robot executes these procedures, SEEK records the measured effects of its actions, including those from failed attempts, in a Persistent Action-Effect Bank (PAEB). Where a retained procedure applies, it acts without a model request; where it meets a condition it does not cover, this evidence guides its revision. On RoboDojo, knowledge retained over ten attempts raises SEEK's average task score from 27.0 to 54.4 and its success rate from 19.7% to 48.0% across all 42 tasks. On 34 of these tasks with GPT-6.2 Astra, SEEK reaches 80.1 after five attempts, against 49.6 for the same model acting directly, and uses 77.6% fewer model requests. this https URL

165. 【2611.33905】ortho-Gaussian++: Suppressing Geometric Drift and Floaters for Robust Gravity-Aligned TDOM Generation in Large-Scale Aerial Scenes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33905

作者：Xiang Wang,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：digital orthophoto maps, true digital orthophoto, aerial scene reconstruction, Gaussian Splatting, demonstrated remarkable performance

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable performance in aerial scene reconstruction, motivating its recent extension to true digital orthophoto maps (TDOMs) generation. We propose Tortho-Gaussian++, a novel 3DGS training framework that suppresses geometric drift and floaters and automatically prunes unreliable peripheral regions, coupled with a robust gravity-aligned TDOM rendering solution. First, we explore geometric support via viewing-ray-based geometric observability analysis, quantitatively characterizing the reliability for each Gaussian and subsequently pruning peripheral regions with particularly weak geometric support. Second, the optimizer state initialization is refined for newly created Gaussians during densification, intrinsically suppressing drift and floaters without external priors. Lastly, a robust gravity direction estimation solution is designed, combining image-derived gravity cues, camera-distribution priors and nadir views, to achieve a gravity-aligned orthogonal projection reference for TDOM rendering. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale UAV (Unmanned Aerial Vehicle) datasets demonstrate that our Tortho-Gaussian++ outperforms vanilla 3DGS by 0.5-3.5 dB in average PSNR and yields comparable or superior performance to prior-guided 3DGS methods. Notably, it reduces the training time, VRAM and Gaussian number by 45.5%, 47.2%, and 58.6%, respectively. Qualitative evaluations confirm that our method effectively suppresses drift and floaters, while robust gravity direction estimation reduces tilt-induced facade artifacts in TDOMs.

166. 【2611.33903】RelPRM: Grounding Visual Process Reward Models in Relational Evidence

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33903

作者：Zhaoxing Zhang,Ziqiu Gui,Cheng Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：detect calculation errors, assign high scores, process reward models, detect calculation, calculation errors

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual process reward models (PRMs) can detect calculation errors yet assign high scores to steps that misread visual relations. Supervision based on subsequent answer success does not directly establish whether a step's relational claim holds. To strengthen visual-relation verification, we first build an automated pipeline that programmatically verifies each claim and identifies the required objects, relations, and image regions. It produces RelProcessTrain and RelProcessBench (RPB), which tests whether scores respond appropriately to relational evidence through controlled image edits and step rephrasings. Building on this pipeline, we further introduce RelPRM, a visual-relation-oriented method for process reward modeling with two training mechanisms. Relation-evidence self-distillation constructs views preserving necessary objects, relations, and dependencies, reducing the burden of finding decisive evidence in complex scenes and transferring reliable evidence-assisted judgments to full images. Relation-evidence-driven consistency training encourages appropriate score changes when relation edits reverse a step's truth, stability under irrelevant edits, and similar score changes across paraphrases. At inference, RelPRM directly scores the current step using only the original image and reasoning context. RelPRM achieves 66.2% on VisualProcessBench (VPB) and 68.4% on RPB, gains of 4.2 and 9.9 percentage points over VisualPRM-8B. These results show that training for visual relations improves both relational verification and step assessment across broader multimodal reasoning tasks.

167. 【2611.33895】WIST: Teacher-Weighted Initial-Noise Spherical Transform for Quality-Preserving Diversity in Distilled Diffusion Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33895

作者：Shayan Baghayi-Nejad,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Timestep-distilled diffusion models, generate high-quality images, reduced mode coverage, Timestep-distilled diffusion, diffusion models generate

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Timestep-distilled diffusion models generate high-quality images in one or a few steps, but often suffer from reduced mode coverage and limited diversity. Recent methods improve diversity by optimizing the initial noise at inference time, requiring costly backpropagation through the denoiser. Moreover, their diversity objectives typically repel generated samples without accounting for how the corresponding teacher model distributes its outputs. We introduce Teacher-Weighted Initial-noise Spherical Transform (TWIST), a lightweight network that transforms the low-frequency components of initial noise to improve diversity without inference-time optimization. TWIST uses a teacher-guided determinantal point process (DPP) objective to align the feature-space diversity of student samples with that of the teacher. By amortizing noise optimization into a single network, TWIST eliminates inference-time backpropagation while preserving the efficiency of few-step generation. On SDXL-Turbo, TWIST matches the diversity of MoNO at less than 25% of its inference cost, while achieving higher recall and better PickScore, ImageReward, and CLIPScore under matched compute budgets. On SDXL-Lightning, it achieves comparable recall at lower cost without the ImageReward degradation observed with MoNO.

168. 【2611.33894】SIA: Selective Image Analogy

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33894

作者：Amirhossein Alimohammadi,Kian Izadpanah,Bardiya Kariminia,Yiorgos Chrysanthou,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：Selective Image Analogy, Image analogy transfers, Image analogy, query image, introduce Selective Image

备注： SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 (Conference Papers). 28 pages including supplementary material. Project page: [this https URL](https://selective-image-analogy.github.io/) Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/alimohammadiamirhossein/selective-image-analogy) Dataset: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/selective-image-analogy/SIA-Multi-Edit-Analogy)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image analogy transfers a demonstrated transformation from a before/after pair (A, A') to a new query image B. Existing methods assume all changes in (A, A') should be fully transferred, limiting flexibility when users want only a subset of the demonstrated edits. We introduce Selective Image Analogy (SIA), a new setting where a single demonstration pair encodes multiple edits, and the user selects at inference time which edits to apply to B. To support this, we curate a multi-edit analogy dataset with full-edit and partial-edit targets. We then train a model that first learns to transfer the complete demonstrated transformation, and then gains selective control through a mask-aligned suppression mechanism: a token-gated LoRA that restricts adapter updates to suppressed edit tokens during denoising, paired with a concept suppression vector that weakens the selected tokens in the text embeddings before denoising. Together, these components allow SIA to suppress unwanted edits that remain visible in A', while preserving the user-selected ones, all without requiring a new demonstration pair for each edit combination. We perform extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations along with user studies to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

169. 【2611.33886】DeltaRAE: Derivative Tokenization for Long Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33886

作者：Mingzhuo Yu,Shengcao Cao,Chengzhi Mao,Yong Cheng,Di Jiang,Lijun Yu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：exist during generation, limit lies, video, static frame, future frames

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Latent video generators are trained on clips of a few seconds, and much of this limit lies in their tokenizers, which spend as many tokens on a static frame as on a moving one and need future frames that do not exist during generation. We introduce DeltaRAE, a causal representation autoencoder for video. Its derivative tokenization encodes periodic anchor frames in full and all remaining frames with a few tokens each, and its causal encoder and decoder read a frozen pretrained image encoder. On UCF-101 and Kinetics-600, a standard diffusion transformer trained on DeltaRAE latents is competitive with state-of-the-art video tokenizers. DeltaRAE halves the tokenizer GFLOPs on 16-frame clips relative to keeping the same number of patch tokens for every frame, and it represents a minute of video with 18.8x fewer tokens. The tokenizer trains end to end on one-minute videos, and in video prediction a generator on its latents produces a full minute of video. The same generator collapses within 15 seconds in most videos when the tokenizer has the same token budget but no pretrained encoder.

Abstract:3D layout-controlled image editing gives users explicit control over object placement and camera viewpoint. We also introduce LayoutLoop, a multi-round 3D layout-controlled image editing benchmark with calibrated layouts and designed revisits. 171. 【2611.33878】Off the Driven Path: Transferable Road Understanding in Driving VLAs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33878

作者：Lena Wild,Christian Helten,Katie Z Luo,Jakob Thumm,Rafael Valencia,Patric Jensfelt,Marco Pavone

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：policies increasingly operate, driving policies increasingly, policies increasingly, increasingly operate, operate without explicit

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:End-to-end driving policies increasingly operate without explicit HD maps at inference, raising the question of what road knowledge they actually internalize. To probe this knowledge through model outputs, we introduce Road-KSU, a framework that automatically converts HD maps and actor annotations into a road-centric scene graph. From this, we derive road-focused question answering and reasoning targets, complemented by map-based trajectory compliance metrics. Pairing Road-KSU with geographically disjoint splits yields a $2 \times 2$ evaluation crossing output modality (language vs. action) with place familiarity (seen vs. unseen roads). On NAVSIM, 95% of test samples lie within 10m of a training sample, and scores of 13 published planners on turns rise steeply with the number of training drives through the test location, pointing to memorized road geometry. Retraining on geographically disjoint splits and probing with Road-KSU confirm that action-space performance degrades most on unfamiliar turns, with failures dominated by geometric compliance. Language accuracy, however, drops on road-centric VQA on straight and curved roads alike, and remains stable on dynamic scene content. Scaling the training data further reveals a double dissociation: more drives through the same places improve driving on unseen roads but leave road question answering unchanged, whereas spreading a fixed data budget over more places increases road question answering with little change in driving performance. These findings show that improved driving scores need not imply broader road understanding on unseen roads, and identify geographic diversity as key to transferable road knowledge.

172. 【2611.33874】X-Matte: VAE-Free Pixel-Space Flow Matching for Single-Step Image Matting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33874

作者：Zanyi Wang,Jiaang Li,Dengyang Jiang,Ziru Wang,Chengzu Li,Davit Abrahamyan,Harry Yang,Serge Belongie,Mengmeng Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Pre-trained generative models, offer rich visual, rich visual priors, Pre-trained generative, additional inference overhead

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pre-trained generative models offer rich visual priors for image matting, but their adaptation often relies on variational autoencoders (VAEs), whose spatial compression can degrade fine alpha details and introduce additional inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose X-Matte, a VAE-free pixel-space flow matching framework for single-step image matting. By eliminating latent compression, X-Matte formulates the RGB-to-alpha transition as a continuous flow directly in pixel space. We adopt trajectory-wide x-prediction with transition-aware alpha supervision, enabling the model to learn from intermediate states while directly predicting the target alpha matte in a single forward pass. This design avoids both VAE encoding and decoding and iterative sampling. Extensive experiments demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art trimap-free matting methods on P3M benchmarks, together with promising zero-shot generalization to RWP-636 and AIM-500. Moreover, X-Matte achieves up to a 9.4x inference speedup over a constructed single-step latent counterpart at high resolution, demonstrating the effectiveness of VAE-free pixel-space modeling for accurate and efficient image matting.

173. 【2611.33870】Concept Generalization in Visual Brain Decoding via Encoder-Generated fMRI

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33870

作者：Peter Yongho Kim,Juhyeon Park,Dongsheng Li,Yansen Wang,Dongqi Han,Mingqing Xiao,Taesup Moon

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：concepts poorly understood, similar semantic distributions, leaving their ability, poorly understood, typically trained

备注： 33 pages, 7 figures, 20 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual brain decoders are typically trained and evaluated on stimuli drawn from similar semantic distributions, leaving their ability to reconstruct unseen concepts poorly understood. We study this concept generalization problem by withholding semantic concepts from a target subject's training data. We find that three recent fMRI-to-image decoders degrade substantially for the held-out concepts, even when the concept remains available for multi-subject models. In contrast, brain encoders that map a pretrained visual representation directly to voxels are markedly more robust to the same semantic holdout. Motivated by this asymmetry, we use such an encoder to generate synthetic fMRI responses for new images and add the resulting image-fMRI pairs to decoder training. Across three decoders, four subjects, and twelve semantic concepts, synthetic data recovers a substantial fraction of the performance lost on unseen concepts without requiring additional, time-consuming fMRI acquisition. We further show that the same approach improves general decoding performance across a wide range of data budgets. Analyses of encoder training conditions, encoder designs, and synthetic-data controls show that the gains depend on both encoder generalization and the semantic content of the synthetic supervision, rather than merely increasing the amount of training data. Together, our results show that encoder-generated fMRI can transfer semantic generalization to decoders.

备注： Accepted at the ECCV 2026 LoViF Workshop. Received the Outstanding Challenge Paper Runner-Up Award

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Raindrops and reflections frequently co-occur on windshields and jointly obscure scene content, making their unified removal, referred to as UR$^3$, a highly ill-posed problem. Existing approaches are mainly based on diffusion models and thus can produce visually plausible restorations. However, they may generate scene-inconsistent structures in severely corrupted regions. To address this limitation, we propose MAFNet-UR$^3$, which adopts a three-stage framework to exploit multiple observations for scene-consistent unified raindrop and reflection removal. Specifically, Stage I suppresses raindrops while preserving the reflection layer, and Stage II performs prior-guided reflection removal followed by image-space refinement. Stage III estimates degradation masks from intermediate restoration results to provide degradation cues and performs mask-aware multi-scale fusion across observations to preserve scene structure and fine details. As a result, our proposed MAFNet-UR$^3$-L achieves state-of-the-art performance on the RDRF-testing benchmark, outperforming DiffUR$^3$ by 2.33 dB in PSNR and 2.7634 in the challenge score. Our method ranked third in the LoViF 2026 UR$^3$ Challenge, achieving a final score of 41.1416.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Audio-visual understanding requires perceptual grounding, temporal understanding, and semantic inference. Multi-teacher distillation can consolidate these capabilities, but direct probability matching does not explicitly capture teacher prediction changes across audio-visual conditions. We introduce OmniMeld, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation method that integrates domain expertise through modality-response-guided distribution matching. We train four teachers covering grounding and correspondence, temporal dynamics and event structure, causal and inferential reasoning, and contextual and holistic understanding. The student improves over its base model across all evaluated benchmarks and surpasses the teachers' base model on several benchmarks.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33860

作者：Zike Luo,Hao Li,Mengxiao Wang,Qingya Li,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：obscure anatomical boundaries, Echocardiographic images, hinder clinical analysis, images are frequently, frequently degraded

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Echocardiographic images are frequently degraded by spatially structured haze artifacts that obscure anatomical boundaries and hinder clinical analysis. Conventional diffusion models typically employ pixel-independent Gaussian noise, limiting their ability to represent localized ultrasound haze. In this study, we propose a structure-informed diffusion framework that explicitly models ultrasound haze as an accumulation of localized Gaussian components constrained by the ultrasound fan geometry. The forward process generates spatially correlated corruption by sampling component locations and scales, providing a formulation that better reflects the spatial characteristics of real echo artifacts. During reverse restoration, a dual-head network predicts an anchor probability map and Gaussian scale parameters to progressively remove structured artifacts while reducing the center bias associated with direct coordinate regression. We evaluated the framework on the public DehazingEcho2025 benchmark and two private clinical datasets, without retraining or fine-tuning on either private cohort. Our method achieved the highest mean generalized contrast-to-noise ratio across all three datasets, reaching $0.715 \pm 0.090$ on the public benchmark while preserving tissue edges.

备注： 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) is essential for robust perception, yet existing methods remain limited under complex degradations. While diffusion models (DMs) offer powerful modeling capabilities to combat such degradations, their iterative process incurs high computational cost. One-step distillation effectively accelerates inference but typically leads to over-smoothed results. To resolve these limitations, we propose a one-step fusion framework with hybrid reinforcement learning, termed OneHRL. We treat degraded inputs as intermediate noisy states along a generative trajectory, enabling a distilled one-step generator to rectify them directly to the clean fusion results. Furthermore, we design a coarse-to-fine reward that integrates a global semantic reward with an object-level reward. This hybrid reward ensures that the generator captures both naturalness and fine-grained texture. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior fusion quality and efficiency.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33843

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Breast ultrasound segmentation models often generalize poorly across scanners, institutions, and patient populations. We investigate continuous foundation-guided pseudo-label weighting for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). An exponential-moving-average (EMA) teacher generates target-domain pseudo-labels, while a frozen ultrasound segmentation model (AutoSAMUS) modulates their contribution using a bounded weight that combines the prior's certainty with its probabilistic agreement with the teacher. Because the weighting rule was developed after some target-test results had been examined, we treat the study as exploratory and report adverse as well as favorable outcomes.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for image segmentation. We propose SPiXSeg, which shifts the output from per-patch to per-superpixel label assignment, using superpixel tokens formed by aggregating patch-level visual features. Our results suggest that a fixed, query-agnostic superpixel partition is enough for accurate language-guided segmentation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper proposes a new paradigm and method for giving large language models spatial understanding. Standard language models operate exclusively over discrete words (secondary representations of things rather than continuous space itself), forcing them to guess geometry verbally. Whereas most multimodal approaches equate spatial grounding with camera images or video frames, spatial structure itself is not a picture: it consists of coordinate frames, solid boundaries, and how objects move and relate to each other in continuous space. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autoregressive text-to-motion generation requires local motion predictions to reconcile cross-temporal textual requirements with the available motion context. While existing methods primarily improve motion representations and generation order, the internal formation of conditioned motion representations remains less explored. We introduce MotionODE, a continuous-depth post-training framework that refines hidden-state evolution in pretrained autoregressive generators.
