点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video anomaly understanding (VAU) requires reasoning about event progression, interactions, and the contextual conditions that distinguish abnormal behavior from common activity across diverse scenes. Although video agents enable multi-round analysis, the predefined analytical operations may not adequately satisfy these diverse reasoning demands. In this paper, we present EvoVAU, a framework that evolves a reusable tool library for a frozen video solver. Guided by task outcomes and execution trajectories, an external proposer identifies potential limitations in the library and refines it, retaining task experience as analytical operations that can be reused across questions. Abstract:World action models (WAMs) support robot control through visual prediction, but dense future modeling repeatedly encodes static context already available in the current observation, while action-relevant changes are often spatially sparse. While action prediction gradients directly drive compact tokens to capture control-relevant cues, dense future guidance further shapes them via action functional guidance that matches sparse and dense contributions to the action expert, and dynamics-aware distribution matching, which aligns temporal relational changes relative to a static reference. 192. 【2611.33796】SprintGS: Eliminating Redundancy for Fast 3D Gaussian Splatting Training

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33796

作者：Ryosuke Watanabe,Tomoaki Konno

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Gaussian Splatting, renders photorealistic, photorealistic novel views, views in real, training

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders photorealistic novel views in real time, but its per-scene training (optimization) remains slow. Recent fast training methods such as FastGS shorten training to a few minutes by reducing the number of Gaussians. Profiling FastGS, we find two costs that remain even in its compact model. First, rasterization is still the largest cost, and redundant work remains in it. Second, the loss computation does not shrink with the number of Gaussians, so its share of the training time grows as the model becomes compact. We present SprintGS, a training framework built on FastGS that keeps the model compact and reduces both costs. For rasterization, scheduled tile culling limits each Gaussian to the tiles where it matters, using a coarse footprint that is annealed to the exact one before training ends; convergence-aware pruning removes converged, nearly transparent Gaussians; and a shuffle-efficient backward pass reduces the data exchanged between GPU threads. For the loss, a fused loss computes the loss and its image gradient in the forward pass without a loss map, removing most of its cost. SprintGS trains 2.15x faster than FastGS at comparable quality, and 1.81x faster in its high-quality setting. It also accelerates other fast training methods and dynamic, sparse-view, and surface reconstruction.

193. 【2611.33794】Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33794

作者：Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Kangan Qian,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：policy collects experience, collects experience, rates it higher, judge, practical recipe

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://zilin-huang.github.io/SealedLoop/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-improvement is now a practical recipe for embodied policies: a policy collects experience, is retrained, and is kept when a judge rates it higher. However, the judge is usually trained or chosen inside the same loop, so its evidence is not independent of the policy. We introduce SealedLoop, an evaluation framework that adds a sealed judge: an evaluator outside the loop that the improver cannot see or optimize against. A new policy is kept only when a sequential test on the sealed judge confirms the gain, with a bounded probability of keeping a worse one. Running the same loop under each judge, we separate two sources of a false gain, which we call judge error (the loop's judge is wrong on its own data) and transfer gap (a real gain there does not carry over to unseen conditions). We evaluate on LIBERO manipulation, NAVSIM driving, and a full-scale real vehicle. In manipulation, a success classifier accepted 65 of 196 failures in one generation. In driving, policies that gain on the loop's own frames lose in an unseen city, so even an exact in-loop judge would keep 8/15 of them. When the sealed test decides, a driving loop built to improve ends with a better policy than under its own critic (0.674 against 0.649 PDMS, from 0.638), and on driving pairs with known sealed gain the test accepted 11 of 12 real gains and none of 59 losses. In a real-world field experiment on a published improvement step, the reinforced version drove further into a curve than the version it started from in all 6 blind pairs, though neither completed it. These results show that evidence from inside the loop, however accurate, is not enough to decide whether a policy has improved. SealedLoop offers a foundation for trustworthy self-improvement of embodied policies, in which progress is judged by evidence the improving system cannot influence.

194. 【2611.33792】Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology via Topology-Informed Graph Attention and Gene-Semantics Conditioning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33792

作者：Yuqi Cheng,Boshuai Zhang,Ziqi Zhang,Xiuwei Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：profiles gene expression, remains costly, motivating models, models that infer, spatial pattern fidelity

备注： Accepted by WACV2027: [this https URL](https://wacv.thecvf.com/virtual/2027/poster/2019)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spatial transcriptomics (ST) profiles gene expression with spatial context but remains costly, motivating models that infer expression from HE images. Existing approaches face three key limitations: (i) a single patch embedding is often insufficient: morphologically similar regions can exhibit distinct expression due to spatial context, while naive neighborhood aggregation can over-smooth boundaries; (ii) conventional fixed, gene-indexed output heads treat genes independently and cannot directly support strict zero-shot prediction for unseen genes; and (iii) common metrics under-measure spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution. To address these challenges, we propose TIGEM, a topology-informed, gene-conditioned framework for histology-to-ST inference. TIGEM learns topology-aware spot representations by regularizing graph attention with tissue-level topology and morphology cues to avoid cross-compartment smoothing, and uses gene semantic modulation to replace gene-indexed heads, enabling zero-shot prediction for unseen genes. We also introduce Graph Spectral Correlation (GSC) to evaluate spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution complement to PCC/MSE. Across three large-scale ST datasets, TIGEM achieves overall state-of-the-art performance and significantly improved boundary prediction resolution, and further demonstrates the ability to predict expression patterns for genes unseen during training, extending prior zero-shot histology-to-ST efforts with topology-aware spatial propagation and PLM-based gene conditioning.

195. 【2611.33790】Bridging Transferability and Discriminability via Positive-Unlabeled Domain Adaptation for Building Damage Mapping Across Disaster Events

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33790

作者：Qirui Wang,Hengwei Zhao,Xiaoshu Yang,Jingtao Li,Bo Li,Sorin Popescu,Rusty Feagin,Wenzhe Jiao

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Mapping the damage to buildings following natural and human-caused disasters is essential for disaster response and humanitarian assistance. However, differences in hazard types, geographic environments, building characteristics, and imaging conditions cause cross-event distribution shifts that limit the generalization of deep learning models to new disaster events, even for geospatial foundation models (GFMs) pretrained on extensive Earth observation data. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) can mitigate these shifts, but adaptation remains challenging when damage-proportion and damage-appearance shifts coexist and interact. We identify enhancing target-domain discriminability as key to addressing this challenge. To this end, inspired by positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, we introduce positive-unlabeled domain adaptation, a paradigm that incorporates PU weak supervision to enhance target-domain discriminability, requiring only a few labeled damaged buildings in the target domain. Building upon this straightforward intuition, we develop Positive-ANchored Domain Adaptation (PANDA), comprising two complementary and mutually reinforcing components: Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness (PAIR) for discriminative representation learning and Refine Gate and Verifier (RAV) for supervision refinement. Further experiments demonstrate the potential of integrating PANDA with GFMs for cross-event adaptation. These findings highlight PANDA's practical potential for rapid, reliable, and cross-event building damage mapping.

Abstract:Medical visual question answering (VQA) requires acquiring useful evidence and deciding where to look next. We introduce MedPACT, an agentic reinforcement learning framework that learns selective observation policies through immediate answer support and prospective guidance. 199. 【2611.33772】riage Before Fine-Tuning: Feasibility-Gated Selection and Monitored Adaptation of Embodied Foundation Models for a Fixed Physical Platform

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33772

作者：Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Zihao Sheng,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Yuhao Luo,Sikai Chen

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Embodied foundation models, local data, Embodied foundation, increasingly deployed, practitioner picks

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://zilin-huang.github.io/Triage/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Embodied foundation models are increasingly deployed by selection: a practitioner picks a public checkpoint by benchmark rank and fine-tunes it on local data. The platform is fixed before the model is chosen, with little or no local data. We ask what such a checkpoint loses on a fixed platform and which part of the loss local data can buy back. We split the gap by what can repair it. A sensing gap, information the sensors do not capture, cannot be closed with their data; a latency gap, output arriving after the control loop needs it, belongs to the loop, not the weights; only a distribution gap is fine-tuning's to repair. TRIAGE turns this into feasibility-gated selection and monitored adaptation in five stages: gates on the sensor contract and on end-to-end latency that no score can override, probes on ten minutes of local logs, a ranking by target-relevant score among the candidates that pass, and fine-tuning that is monitored and checked in closed loop before release. On driving benchmarks, a deadline below a leading closed-loop model's latency makes it collide on 80 to 90% of routes, and losing field of view moves plans 2.2 to 5.7 times more than losing resolution at equal pixels. Local frames close 75% of a stability gap but raise perturbation sensitivity at small budgets, and picking by score violates a gate in two of four deployment profiles. On a full-scale vehicle, the factory camera failed the sensing gate and an added camera repaired it. The latency gate removed the two highest-scoring candidates, neither of which completed a closed-loop trip. The selected model passed acceptance in 4 of 5 closed-loop trips, and the closed-loop check withheld a fine-tuned model that had improved offline. TRIAGE lets organizations that cannot train foundation models from scratch find out whether a public checkpoint can run on the robot or vehicle they own, and what to fix if not.

200. 【2611.33761】ScanAR: Parallel Row-Scan Decoding for Efficient Image Generation in Unified Autoregressive Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33761

作者：Jingen Qu,Xi Chen,Yingbo Tang,Xinghang Li,Tao Kong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：shared next-token prediction, models represent text, Unified multimodal autoregressive, next-token prediction head, represent text

备注： 12 pages, 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Unified multimodal autoregressive (AR) models represent text and images in one token sequence and generate all modalities with a shared next-token prediction head. Their image generation, however, decodes an $H\times W$ visual grid token by token in raster order, which takes $HW$ sequential steps and dominates inference time. Existing acceleration methods for visual AR generation either change the tokenizer, the prediction head, or the model interface, or keep the model but cannot reach high parallelism without losing quality, so they do not carry over to unified AR models. We propose ScanAR, a row-scan parallel decoding method that only redefines the predecessors of visual tokens. The first visual row is generated from left to right as usual, and each later row is generated in a single parallel step, with every token predicted from the vertically aligned token of the previous row. Nonvisual tokens keep the standard autoregressive rule. ScanAR reduces the visual decoding depth from $HW$ to $H+W$ without changing the backbone, vocabulary, or output head and without adding parameters, and it supports both training from scratch and lightweight post-training. On Emu3.5-Image-34B, ScanAR post-training reaches a GenEval score of 82.36, the best among post-training acceleration methods trained on the same data, and generates $512\times512$ images $13.8\times$ faster than raster decoding under the same inference setting. On ImageNet, ScanAR trained from scratch has the highest throughput at every model size with FID comparable to raster LlamaGen. On the 34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 model, ScanAR preserves the overall quality of embodied scene generation, interleaved image-text generation, and video generation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Wearable assistants must localize interface elements from camera observations when direct screenshot access is unavailable, yet existing GUI grounding benchmarks primarily evaluate native screenshots. We introduce GlassGround, a new benchmark that pairs native screenshots with wearable camera observations while holding GUI states, instructions, and targets fixed. GlassGround contains 3,250 tasks across Android and iOS, captured using four wearable devices under four viewing conditions. Across ten open-source models, mean grounding accuracy drops from 94.8% on screenshots to 48.5% on camera images. Further analysis reveals that changes in target scale, screen geometry, and background cannot fully explain this performance degradation. We then study two ways to narrow this gap. At inference time, local zooming and screen rectification help but leave most of the gap. For training-time adaptation, we build on GlassGround's paired-view design and introduce PairDistill, a baseline that aligns target representations across views alongside coordinate supervision. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Change detection identifies where images differ, but in practice users often seek only a particular kind of change. Existing referring change detection methods rely on predefined categories, textual descriptions, or annotated masks, limiting flexibility or requiring additional annotation. In this paper, we propose a new way to perform change detection, where an unannotated before-and-after image pair serves as a visual example to guide change localization in another pair. 203. 【2611.33751】VAPOR: Improving Multimodal Reasoning with Sparse Visual Updates

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33751

作者：Mingyu Su,Wei Wu,Erle Zhu,Chengxing Xie,Deyao Hong,Hongning Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Multimodal large language, large language models, Multimodal large, made substantial progress, large visual contexts

备注： 19 pages, including appendix. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/Su-my/VAPOR)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in visual reasoning, but extended generation over large visual contexts remains computationally expensive. During autoregressive decoding, visual attention is recomputed for every generated token, even when consecutive tokens rely on similar visual evidence. We introduce VAPOR (Visual Attention Persistence with On-demand Refresh), a training-free framework that reduces this redundancy by reusing visual attention outputs across decoding steps and refreshing them when needed. VAPOR caches the visual attention output while continuing to update textual attention at every step, helping reduce visual grounding drift during extended reasoning. To decide when to reuse cached outputs, VAPOR estimates visual attention mass using a low-rank approximation. It also uses prefill-based attention consistency and cache age to make independent Persist and Refresh decisions for each attention head. VAPOR retains the full visual key-value (KV) cache, so all visual tokens remain available when new evidence is needed. Experiments on eight image and four video reasoning benchmarks across two MLLM families show that VAPOR improves accuracy over dense attention on nearly all benchmarks while achieving 1.11-1.21x decoding speedups. Compared with the strongest state-of-the-art acceleration baseline in each setting, VAPOR further improves accuracy by 0.35-2.15 percentage points while achieving comparable or lower decoding latency. Our code is available at this https URL.

204. 【2611.33750】Spectral Colorimetry Based on GMoE for Temperature Measurement in Explosion Fields

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33750

作者：Jie Fang,Wenhao Li,Jianning Yang,Nan Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：related engineering domains, high-temperature measurement scenarios, attracted considerable attention, traditional contact methods, Spectroscopic thermometry

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spectroscopic thermometry in explosion fields has attracted considerable attention in related engineering domains, primarily because it can mitigate the interference of traditional contact methods with temperature distributions and address the limitations of high-temperature measurement scenarios. However, existing physics-model-driven methods typically rely on regressing a single radiance-ratio pair, which limits their adaptability to wide-range temperature estimation. This limitation arises from notable variations in the mapping between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios at different temperatures. In contrast, existing data-driven methods often suffer from limited performance due to the scarcity of training data, a challenge exacerbated by the sensitivity of explosion fields. In this work, we propose a gated mixture of experts (GMoE) framework that integrates a divide-and-conquer strategy in deep learning with physical prior knowledge for temperature estimation, thereby decomposing the complex task into multiple expert-specific subtasks. Specifically, each expert is developed using physically calibrated polynomial functions to model the relationship between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios for a given pair of spectral bands, with temperature then estimated using a physical model incorporating the corresponding parameters. Additionally, a gating network is designed to adaptively weight the outputs of different experts and generate the final temperature estimate. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves the best performance when implemented with the Transformer-based gating network, yielding a mean RMSE of $12.70 \pm 1.32$ K and a mean MAE of $7.96 \pm 0.86$ K. Compared with conventional spectral colorimetry, the proposed framework reduces the mean RMSE and MAE by 63.4\% and 70.8\%, respectively.

205. 【2611.33749】aching Students to Stand on Their Own: Distilling Vision Foundation Models for Fine-Grained Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33749

作者：Junsu Kim,Seungryul Baek

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：incrementally learn visually, learn visually similar, few-shot class-incremental learning, class-incremental learning, aims to incrementally

备注： pre-print version

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Fine-grained few-shot class-incremental learning (FG-FSCIL) aims to incrementally learn visually similar new classes from limited images without catastrophic forgetting. Prior work typically learns strong representations on base classes or leverages vision foundation models (VFMs) directly as backbones or via knowledge distillation. However, VFM backbones incur high computational costs and do not guarantee fine-grained recognition. Although knowledge distillation reduces inference cost, our preliminary experiments reveal that distilled students still lag substantially behind their VFM teachers, particularly on new classes. To address this limitation, we propose STAND, a systematic VFM-to-student distillation design tailored for FG-FSCIL. Specifically, STAND connects the teacher and student by equipping the student with a fixed copy of the teacher's classifier, aligning their outputs via projectors, and feeding both models composite images that combine different training images. On these composite images, relation distillation matches how the teacher and the student relate each image to other images, while difference distillation matches how this relation changes between two composite images. Finally, we freeze the student and classify new classes using length-calibrated prototypes, which restore the reduced similarity of few-shot prototypes. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that STAND achieves state-of-the-art performance across FG-FSCIL datasets at low computational cost, while generalizing across student backbones and broader datasets.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in world models for game interaction and control have increased the demand for large-scale gameplay videos paired with camera trajectories. However, capturing synchronized camera trajectories often requires game-specific pipelines, limiting scalability. We therefore propose GameWeaver, a unified diffusion framework that automatically produces paired video--camera data through three complementary tasks: camera pose estimation from observed video, video generation along a prescribed camera trajectory, and joint video--camera generation from a reference image and text. First, each tipxel stores the normalized 3D coordinates of a pixel ray's endpoint in a reference coordinate frame. The resulting three-channel tipxel fields and RGB frames share the pretrained video model's VAE. Next, a fully trainable dual-stream Transformer exchanges appearance and geometry features through bidirectional gated attention and conditions both streams on shared text features. Finally, deterministic fitting recovers camera intrinsics and normalized trajectories from generated tipxel fields. In our evaluation tasks, GameWeaver achieves a normalized translation MSE of 0.0029 for camera control; for joint generation, it scores 85.48 out of 100 on instruction adherence versus 64.71 for the best cascade on that metric, validating the feasibility of the proposed approach.

207. 【2611.33725】VTK-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Distinguishing Language Priors and Text Modal Bias in Visual Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33725

作者：Yao Lu,Yuhang Zheng,Yaxin Gao,Zhe Tang,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei,Yunhao Wang,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：demonstrated exceptional capabilities, undermining their reliability, demonstrated exceptional, exceptional capabilities, failure modes

备注： 25 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual language models (VLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities across various multimodal tasks, but they remain susceptible to hallucinations, undermining their reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks primarily focus on identifying hallucinations and describing their manifestations, often neglecting their underlying causes. Specifically, multimodal hallucinations can stem from two distinct failure modes: text modality bias (TMB), reflecting an over-reliance on misleading text input; and language prior (LP), reflecting a reliance on previously learned knowledge. To differentiate these failure modes, we propose VTK-Bench, a benchmark that explicitly separates three information sources: visual information (V), textual information (T), and prior knowledge (K). By establishing mutually exclusive answers corresponding to V, T, and K, VTK-Bench enables fine-grained attribution analysis of model responses. Furthermore, we introduce discrete attribution and soft attribution metrics to characterize information source preferences. Extensive experiments on 11 representative open-source VLMs show that LP is more prevalent than TMB in most evaluated models. Notably, TMB decreases substantially with model scaling, whereas LP exhibits relatively limited improvement and remains the dominant failure mode. These findings highlight the importance of different information source preferences for a deeper understanding of multimodal hallucinations.

Abstract:Automated currency denomination recognition is a computer vision task with applications in cash handling, financial technology, and assistive systems. The study shows the feasibility of combining lightweight deep learning with a voice-assisted web interface for Nepali currency recognition, and highlights the need for more diverse datasets, greater robustness under uncontrolled imaging conditions, and broader real-world evaluation.

209. 【2611.33698】Efficient Scene-Distribution-Aware Second-Order Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33698

作者：Hyunseung Son,Young Sun Choi,Youngsik Yun,Kibeom Hong,Youngjung Uh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Gaussian Splatting, Gaussian parameters, Gaussian, tens of thousands, Splatting

备注： 16 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Accepted to ACCV 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) optimization updates the Gaussian parameters one training view at a time, tens of thousands of times. Unlike such single-view updates, multi-view batches reduce dependence on individual training views, and second-order optimization reduces the number of updates with larger, structured parameter changes. Because fewer updates leave fewer chances to correct a change that fits only the views used in one update, scene coverage becomes more important for each second-order update. Multi-view second-order updates, however, are expensive. Pixel sampling reduces this cost, but two issues remain. First, selecting views without considering overlap leaves some views redundant. To address this, we propose a view selection strategy that reduces this redundancy. Second, existing work processes the sampled pixels of each view in a separate pass and recomputes alpha compositing for these pixels from scratch. We introduce two implementation strategies that reduce both overheads by processing the sampled pixels of multiple views in joint passes and reusing the transmittance and accumulated color cached during rendering. On three datasets, our method reaches higher PSNR than the sampled second-order baseline in less than half its optimization time, with competitive SSIM and LPIPS.

215. 【2611.33655】Recall4D: Forgetting, Not Misalignment, Limits Long-Horizon Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33655

作者：Tianle Lyu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：short video clips, long videos degrades, video clips, short video, long videos

备注： 20 pages, 10 figures, 15 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://kincin.github.io/Recall4D/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward 4D trackers recover camera motion, depth, and point trajectories from short video clips, but chaining windows over long videos degrades their accuracy. We find that replacing inter-window alignment with ground-truth transforms leaves tracking error essentially unchanged. Static-point error instead grows with time since last observation: repeatedly predicting a static point from windows that no longer observe it corrupts previously recovered geometry. We call this failure forgetting. Merely retaining the last observed positions of unseen points reduces error by 26% on PointOdyssey without modifying the tracker. We introduce Recall4D, a training-free framework that augments a frozen tracker with a persistent world state and sparse motion anchors. Static points retain their world-space positions outside the camera view and are refined by averaging observations decoded at sparse keyframes while in view. For dynamic objects, the tracker decodes sparse representative anchors, while geometric skinning propagates their displacements to dense queries. Across two long-horizon benchmarks, Recall4D improves the accuracy of its backbone while running 7.6-9.0$\times$ faster, and its accuracy gains carry over to five further backbones and three other long-sequence tasks, demonstrating its generality.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33636

作者：Dongheon Lee,DongJae Lee,Sewhan Chun,Chanseul Cho,Youngwook Kim,Youngmin Ro,Yonghyun Jeong

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-scale architectures are widely adopted in all-in-one image restoration (AIO-IR) to capture larger-scale context at low-resolution stages and refine details at high-resolution (HR) stages. However, processing spatially large feature maps at HR stages incurs substantial computational costs and memory traffic, making efficient HR processing challenging. To address this challenge, we propose High-resolution Global-Local Restoration (HiGLR), an efficient multi-scale AIO-IR network grounded in an analysis of spatial redundancy in self-attention and convolutional feed-forward networks (ConvFFNs). Specifically, this analysis identifies three key redundancies: per-query readout at the finest-scale stage, key routing across neighboring queries at intermediate stages, and spatial mixing in the expanded feature spaces of ConvFFNs. Accordingly, HiGLR replaces costly self-attention at the finest-scale stage with a learned input-independent kernel. At intermediate stages, we propose a Block-Routed Hybrid Attention, which combines coarse-scale dense attention with fine-scale block-sparse attention over value residuals relative to coarse summaries, and shares routing indices across neighboring queries. We further introduce a Dual-Space FFN that performs spatial mixing in a compact space and pointwise nonlinear transformation in an expanded space, enabling efficient kernel fusion. Combining these designs, HiGLR achieves state-of-the-art performance while delivering up to a $4.24\times$ inference speedup over existing methods.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted for end-to-end driving planning, yet their performance is typically evaluated based on a single greedily decoded trajectory. However, greedy decoding may overlook better trajectories within the model's output distribution. On WOD-E2E, when the ego vehicle should start moving, the model often assigns probability to different possible start times, causing greedy decoding to repeatedly favor waiting and produce a stationary plan even though moving trajectories are available. 222. 【2611.33615】Learning from Natural Images Untangles Number Sense

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33615

作者：Yechan Cho,Hyeonsu Lee,Emma Deneuville,Jaeson Jang

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computational Geometry (cs.CG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Neurons and Cognition (q-bio.NC)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Numerical cognition is a simple yet fundamental ability that remains challenging for modern vision-language models. Meanwhile, number sense is already present in numerically naïve animals, suggesting that it can emerge without explicit numerical learning. Deep neural networks provide a related observation, with numerosity-selective units appearing after training on unrelated natural-image tasks and even before any training. However, these findings leave unresolved whether visual learning contributes beyond the spontaneous emergence of numerical selectivity. Human development provides a clue, as children become better at making judgments in which numerosity and proportion conflict. Here, we introduce a geometric framework to trace how representations of these two quantities change across learning. Although untrained and pretrained networks exhibited similar unit-level numerosity selectivity and basic comparison performance, natural-image pretraining increased the angle between numerosity and proportion subspaces while reducing their overlap, supporting more robust readout under conflict. Direct manipulation of these geometric properties further demonstrated their functional contributions to decoding accuracy and functional independence. Importantly, conventional supervised pretraining was not essential for quantity separation, which also emerged through self-supervised learning without category labels and synthetic-image training without natural-image exposure. Together, our findings suggest that structured visual learning can refine spontaneously emerging number sense into robust and functionally independent quantity representations without explicit numerical training.

224. 【2611.33588】SEEL: Towards End-to-End 3D Sign Generation with Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33588

作者：Ronglai Zuo,Fei Ni,Junpeng Jing,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias,Jiankang Deng,Stefanos Zafeiriou

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：produce signing motion, language generation aims, sign language, aims to produce, produce signing

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D sign language generation aims to produce signing motion from written text. Motivated by the linguistic nature of sign language, recent methods combine a motion tokenizer with a language model to predict motion tokens from text. However, both components are optimized through a two-stage pipeline with mismatched objectives: the tokenizer minimizes reconstruction error in isolation, while the generator learns from rigid one-hot reference tokens. A straightforward end-to-end extension bridges discrete token selection with straight-through estimators (STE), suffering from biased gradients and codebook collapse. To address these limitations, we propose SEEL, an end-to-end framework that aligns tokenization and generation with motion quality through two complementary mechanisms. First, to overcome rigid one-hot supervision, reconstruction-aware generation decodes candidate tokens in context and converts their 3D motion errors into soft targets, guiding the generator with physical motion quality. Second, to propagate generation feedback without STE, generation-aware tokenization passes continuous codebook expectations through the frozen generator, routing generated-motion gradients to the tokenizer to prevent codebook collapse. Combining both mechanisms enables the tokenizer and generator to co-evolve, achieving state-of-the-art performance on CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and Phoenix-2014T. Code and models will be released to facilitate future research.

Abstract:Recent generative video methods achieve strong perceptual compression by encoding videos into compact latent representations and reconstructing them with powerful generative decoders. 作者：Pureum Kim,Younggeon Ryu,Dongyoon Lee,Hae Beom Lee,Kyong Hwan Jin

关键词：Implicit neural representations, capture global low-frequency, global low-frequency structure, localized high-frequency details, refine localized high-frequency

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Implicit neural representations (INRs) capture global low-frequency structure during the early stage of training and then refine localized high-frequency details, afterwards. However, standard optimizers are agnostic to the coarse-to-fine learning behavior of INRs. Such optimizers accumulate a gradient matrix in momentum buffer and retain components misaligned with the dominant low-frequency directions in the early stage. We propose a progressively growing rank scheduler and a rank-aware learning-rate scheduler for enhancing a subspace-based momentum optimization, where a momentum matrix is updated within a low-rank subspace. The rank schedule expands the subspace during training, and the scheduler holds the peak learning rate through rank growth so that the added subspace contributes to fine-detail recovery. Experiments on image fitting, single-image super-resolution (SISR), and neural radiance field optimization show that the proposed method improves convergence and peak reconstruction quality over competitive optimizers. The largest gains appear in the high-frequency refinement stage, mirroring the coarse-to-fine progression that the rank schedule targets. A significant study on hyperparameter optimization (HPO) on NeRF further confirms that these gains persist under matched search budgets.

228. 【2611.33565】EgoCARBS: Egocentric Video Meets Active Acoustics for Kitchen Action Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33565

作者：Saif Mahmud,Vineet Parikh,Bharath Hariharan,Cheng Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：Egocentric video records, leave the frame, Egocentric video, exocentric camera, hands leave

备注： 36 pages (12 main paper and references, 24 supplementary material). Project page: [this https URL](https://saif-mahmud.github.io/projects/ego-carbs)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Egocentric video records what a person looks at, but often misses what their body does: the hands leave the frame, and arm motion goes unseen. An exocentric camera recovers this context, but it must be installed wherever the activity happens. Active acoustic sensing can recover it from the wearables themselves: a device's speaker emits inaudible chirps, and its microphone senses the motion of the hands, arms, and nearby objects from their echoes. Yet no public dataset pairs it with egocentric video. We present EgoCARBS, with two deliverables. The first is a multimodal dataset and benchmark: 15 participants cook unscripted meals in their own kitchens wearing camera glasses and an off-the-shelf smartwatch that both sense with active acoustics, synchronized with egocentric and exocentric video and the watch's audio and IMU, with 4,019 verb-noun action annotations in 11.4 hours. The second is a study of what the wearables add to egocentric video. On verb recognition, they add at least as much as an exocentric camera for participants seen in training, and even more for new participants, for whom the exocentric camera does not help. Active acoustics alone provides most of this gain, and helps most where the camera does not see the hands. Trimmed action recognition, detection, and anticipation remain open. We will release the data, our data collection and annotation tools, and pre-trained models.

229. 【2611.33539】Frame geometry, fascicle extrapolation and leaderboard overfitting in muscle ultrasound

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33539

作者：Soheil Kooklan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：B-mode ultrasound depend, add settings chosen, thickness from B-mode, B-mode ultrasound, entries add settings

备注： 38 pages (20 main text, 18 supplement), 13 figures, 21 tables. Code, trained weights, notebooks and derived data are public on Kaggle (user soheilkooklan) and in the ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Automated readings of pennation angle, fascicle length and muscle thickness from B-mode ultrasound depend on the geometry of the stored frame and on how fascicles that leave the image are extended, and challenge entries add settings chosen on a small public leaderboard. This study measures all three in one entry of the UMUD Challenge, which scored 309 frames from six screen layouts, 103 on a public and 206 on a private leaderboard. The pipeline applies layout-specific values of scale and pixel aspect, resamples each frame to square pixels, segments aponeuroses and fascicle fragments with U-Nets and measures the three readings geometrically; the submitted entry blended its angle and length with public notebooks. Because the score is additive, 65 single-change contrasts from 13 base entries measured each decision on both splits. The primary selected entry scored 0.27608 on the private split, the 69th reported position of 690 teams. Reading every frame with square pixels cost 0.029 public and 0.037 private. Of 20 late-stage corrections, 9 kept the sign of their effect on the private split. An audit of the UMUD expert benchmark found that 26 of its 35 frames share their tissue with training images of both DL_Track_US and the challenge. On the 9 frames with no detected match in either set or orientation, the recipe scored 0.213, two experts against two others 0.218 and DL_Track_US 0.263 (0.209, 0.196 and 0.259 when both raters of a reference pair are required), with no difference established. Frame geometry and the extension rule moved readings as much as the choice of model, and small corrections tuned on about 100 public frames did not carry over.

230. 【2611.33532】AVERT: Anomalous Value Erasure with Response Testing against Visual Prompt Injection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33532

作者：Newt Hue-Nam K. Nguyen,Jui-Yu Ho,Wei-Han Chen,Pin-Yen Ting,Yangqiu Song,Shang-Tse Chen,Jun-Cheng Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Vision Language Models, Vision Language, malicious tool calls, attacker chosen content, perturbed image forces

备注： 21 pages, 13 figures, 15 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision Language Models (VLMs) are vulnerable to visual prompt injection, in which an adversarially perturbed image forces the model to respond with the attacker's chosen content, such as malicious tool calls, website links, or advertisements, sometimes regardless of the user's question. Existing defenses, such as diffusion-based purification and image-token masking, mitigate these attacks at a substantial cost to the model's utility on benign inputs. To address this challenge, we propose AVERT, which removes adversarial feature directions only when the model's answer depends on them. Our key procedures involve identifying anomalous value subspaces and detecting answer dependence. To identify the anomalous value subspace, we compare the statistics of each input's attention-head outputs with those of clean images and extract the directions along which the input deviates the most. We then project out these directions to remove the injected content. Because the same directions also carry benign visual information, we apply the removal only to answers that depend on them, namely answers whose likelihood collapses once the directions are removed or that are as likely under unrelated questions as under the user's question. Against white-box attacks on four VLMs (Qwen2-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, LLaVA, InternVL2.5) and four injection benchmarks, AVERT lowers average attack success from 86-90% to at most 0.6% while retaining at least 96% of undefended utility averaged over thirteen benchmarks. Its defense-utility F-score ($F_{DU}$), the harmonic mean of attacks stopped and utility retained, is 98.0-99.1 and the highest among all compared defenses on every model.

231. 【2611.33511】ArtLang-4D: Articulation-Aware Dynamic Language Fields from Monocular Videos

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33511

作者：Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：door opened halfway, push the drawer, drawer pulled, language field, opened halfway

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language that describes an articulated object is often about its state: the drawer pulled out the furthest, the door opened halfway, push the drawer all the way in. Existing 3D language fields attach one feature to each point and cannot tell these descriptions apart, and 4D language fields condition on video time, which has no kinematic meaning and cannot be set by a user. We present ArtLang-4D, which reconstructs an articulated object from a single monocular RGB-D video and equips it with a dynamic language field indexed by articulation time t, the normalized state of its joints, instead of video time. The geometry is a canonical triangle field whose triangles follow typed screw joints, discovered from the video without any joint initialization. The language field is supervised by a t-tagged caption bank that describes each part with comparative state phrases in a whitened open-vocabulary text space. A free-form query is answered by maximizing part relevance jointly over parts and states, which returns both the part and the articulation state it describes; a command is then executed by moving only that part along its recovered joint. On the 100 objects of Articulate-100, ArtLang-4D recovers more accurate joints and part geometry than reconstruction and articulation baselines, retrieves the queried state far more reliably than a state-agnostic language field, and executes language commands with lower motion error.

232. 【2611.33507】Separating Environment and Sensor Shift from Acquisition Protocol in Physical Vision Benchmarks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33507

作者：Yuyang Yuan,Jason Wu,Mohammadali Khodabandehlou,Jianpeng Yao,Hang Qiu,Mani Srivastava

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：real-world deployments, vision systems, systems must operate, varying environmental conditions, environmental conditions

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In real-world deployments, vision systems must operate under varying environmental conditions, such as illumination changes. Recent work has explored adaptive sensing strategies that select camera parameters according to the current environment and downstream task to improve task performance. Reliable physical benchmarks are therefore essential for evaluating such strategies. We study ImageNet-ES-Diverse, a physical benchmark that evaluates the adaptive exposure control strategy Lens by recapturing printed ImageNet images under varying illumination conditions. We construct four controlled acquisition pipelines that vary printed-image scale and camera zoom, and conduct a pilot study with directly captured physical objects. We find that the acquisition choices substantially change benchmark difficulty on all downstream baselines. Moreover, the average classification-accuracy advantage of Lens over auto exposure observed on the original benchmark does not persist in either our collected pipelines or real-object pilot study. Oracle exposure selection nevertheless retains substantial gains, indicating remaining potential for sensor control. These findings highlight acquisition protocol as an important factor in physical benchmark evaluation and motivate explicit protocol reporting and evaluation across multiple acquisition setups when assessing adaptive-sensing benefits.

233. 【2611.33498】A 3D Simulator for Passive SPAD Imaging

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33498

作者：Elia Mergaert,Rong Zou,Roberto Pellerito,Simone Nascivera,Davide Scaramuzza

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Passive single-photon avalanche, single-photon avalanche diode, sensors remains difficult, Passive single-photon, collecting task annotations

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Passive single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras provide short-exposure observations for low-light perception, but collecting task annotations for these sensors remains difficult, and annotated datasets are scarce. We present a 3D simulation pipeline that generates realistic photon detections together with camera-motion and semantic labels, enabling perception models to be trained on synthetic SPAD observations and applied directly to real-world data. The pipeline couples controllable scene geometry and motion with a sensor-informed observation model that accounts for photon shot noise, dark counts, and spatial crosstalk. We evaluate the pipeline through calibrated photon-count comparisons and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across three downstream perception tasks. We show that classifiers trained only on synthetic SPAD data achieve high accuracy on real low-light recordings at illumination levels less than 2 lux, without real-data fine-tuning. Similarly, we apply models trained entirely on simulated camera sweeps to real-world camera-rotation estimation and achieve mean absolute rotation errors of less than 1 degree on real 20-degree and 30-degree sweeps at 0.5 and 5.0 lux, when deployed zero-shot. We additionally train semantic segmentation models on synthetic data with automatically generated dense labels and perform direct inference on real-world low-light recordings; the results further demonstrate the usefulness of synthetic dense supervision for real low-light perception. Our simulator thus provides a practical source of annotated training data for real SPAD perception.

234. 【2611.33492】EvOrderBench: When do sampled frames establish event order?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33492

作者：Xiaoqin Feng,Yuqi Li,Xinyuan Li,Neil Rodrigues,Xianglong Wang,Kuiye Ding,Yingli Tian

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：frames, Vision-language models, order, video happened, Vision-language

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked which of two actions in a video happened first, given only a few sampled frames. Such frames can show that both actions occurred without showing which came first, so a reliable model should state an order only when the frames establish it. Forced-choice temporal benchmarks treat every input as answerable and reward lucky guesses, while answerability benchmarks do not separate frames that miss an action from frames that show both actions but not their order. We introduce EvOrderBench, a real-video benchmark of 579 event families. Each family fixes an episode and a question and varies only the frames: of two four-frame inputs, only one establishes the order, and two six-frame inputs extend the other one with either the frames that resolve the order or frames that do not. Every input is labeled for whether its frames establish each action and their order. Across sixteen open-weight configurations and three closed-source references, the best open models order nearly as accurately as the references when the frames establish the order, but withhold correctly on at most 83% of the inputs where they do not, against at least 90% for every reference; forced-choice accuracy cannot reveal this gap. Open models withhold mainly when the frames show neither action; once the frames show one or both, most of them commit to an order. Thinking narrows the gap, even when it is switched on within a single checkpoint, mainly by separating sufficient from insufficient evidence rather than by making models more cautious overall. Accurate ordering thus does not imply knowing when the frames fall short, and benchmarks should reward withholding alongside ordering.

235. 【2611.33475】Memory as the Backbone: A Dual-Store Associative Memory Architecture for Visual Recognition

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33475

作者：Ruizhi Wang,Xiangtao Wang,Bayar Menzat,Jianfeng Wang,Thomas Lukasiewicz

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：general-purpose visual backbone, memory, Dual-Store Associative Memory, general-purpose visual, visual backbone

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We investigate whether explicit memory can serve as the organizing principle of a general-purpose visual backbone. Inspired by the interaction between working and long-term memory in human cognition, we introduce DuSAM (Dual-Store Associative Memory), a hierarchical memory-native visual backbone coupling image-local working memory (WM) with a fixed-capacity persistent semantic memory (PM). WM organizes local visual evidence into compact, spatially anchored states that are updated and transported across stages, then read back to refine dense features. PM consolidates semantic evidence from training images and annotated regions into prototypes, whose retrieved content enriches the same working states. With these memory operations in place of self-attention or state-space blocks, DuSAM achieves competitive performance relative to representative, similarly sized backbones on ImageNet-1K classification, ADE20K semantic segmentation, and COCO object detection and instance segmentation. Ablations show contributions from both stores, with PM particularly benefiting class groups with fewer annotated training pixels. At $2048\times2048$, the complete DuSAM-based UPerNet segmentor achieves $2.36\times$ the throughput of the evaluated DeiT-S/16 counterpart while using $59.5\%$ less GPU memory, and $1.24\times$ the throughput of the linear-complexity VMamba-T. Memory analyses reveal spatially selective WM access and semantic associations between retrieved prototypes and training regions. Class-targeted removal and restoration further demonstrate that category-specific experience can be replenished through state updates without changing network parameters. These results establish explicit memory as a viable organizing principle for visual backbones and provide a foundation for jointly designing contextual computation and addressable semantic memory.

236. 【2611.33474】Streaming Tax: Can Streaming VLMs Replace Their Offline Parents?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33474

作者：Zhi Yang,Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Most vision-language models (VLMs) specialize in offline video understanding; in contrast, their post-trained versions, streaming VLMs, can perform live interactions. We investigate whether streaming VLMs can replace their offline parent models on offline tasks. Such replacement could reduce deployment costs and improve efficiency. We propose Streaming Tax to systematically evaluate streaming VLMs on offline tasks. Our exploration spans three core dimensions: understanding, trustworthiness, and generalization across 15 benchmarks. Our findings reveal a critical gap between offline capability preservation and practical replaceability. This gap emerges at two stages: streaming adaptation can alter trustworthy behavior even when offline understanding is largely preserved, while the native streaming protocol can introduce further performance losses. Our analyses show that some of these losses arise from restricted access to historical visual evidence and failures to deliver usable responses. Importantly, regarding safety, streaming models often leave both harmful and legitimate requests unanswered rather than genuinely refusing unsafe ones. We then propose Stream++, a training-free plugin to improve streaming VLMs on offline tasks.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33467

作者：Frank Tsu-Fang Lu,Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield

备注： 21 pages, 15 figures, 13 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

Abstract:Digital signers, also known as signing avatars, convey sign language through animated virtual humans, where visual fidelity and linguistic intelligibility hinge primarily on the hands. 239. 【2611.33462】FoamSurf: A Voronoi-Surfel Representation for Differentiable Rendering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33462

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Scenes inside a scattering medium contain solid surfaces, such as the seabed or rocks, and a semi-transparent medium, such as water, fog, or smoke. Recent Gaussian-based methods produce realistic images of scenes in scattering media, but traces of the medium can remain visible in surface-only renders. Neural fields separate surfaces and medium more accurately, but they are slow to train and difficult to edit. Both approaches evaluate the medium only along camera rays, so its effects along reflected and refracted rays are not modeled. These limitations prevent reliable surface recovery, independent editing of surfaces and the medium, and consistent rendering of inserted objects. We present FoamSurf, a hybrid representation that models surfaces with planar Gaussian surfels and the medium with space-filling Voronoi cells. A single differentiable ray tracer composites both along arbitrary rays, while training priors keep them separate without segmentation masks. Across real underwater scenes, synthetic scenes, and dense fog, FoamSurf achieves image quality on par with the state of the art and the most accurate surface-medium separation among the compared methods. The recovered components can be removed, edited, or stylized independently, and the same reconstruction supports inserted mesh objects with attenuation and scattering along both direct and reflected or refracted rays. Together, these capabilities enable scene restoration, editing, and composition within a single reconstructed model. Project page: this https URL

241. 【2611.33455】Recovering What Matters, Transferring Only What Is Reliable: Toward Precise Reference-Based Super-Resolution

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33455

作者：Sihan Ji,Wenxin Chen,Yue Shi,Jiezhang Cao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Reference-based super-resolution, recover missing details, aims to recover, recover missing, Reference-based

备注： 15 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reference-based super-resolution (RefSR) aims to recover missing details from high-quality references. However, existing methods face two fundamental challenges. First, perceptual plausibility does not guarantee content fidelity. Existing methods often produce visually convincing details without faithfully recovering reference-specific information, such as text, codes, and identity cues. Second, faithful reference reading does not guarantee reliable information transfer. Increasing reference utilization also exposes the restoration process to misleading content, as global semantic relevance does not necessarily imply local correspondence consistency. To address these challenges, we propose PreciseRef, a reference-aware generative framework for faithful and reliable RefSR. At its core, Asymmetric Twin-Attention (ATA) allows target and reference images to participate in a unified attention space while the reference keeps its own shape. By letting their image tokens interact directly through joint attention, ATA facilitates fine-grained reference information retrieval without explicit image alignment. To ensure reliable information transfer, we introduce Dual-Scale Reference Gating (DRG), which combines Semantic Relevance Gating (SRG) to filter globally irrelevant reference context and Bidirectional Consistency Gating (BCG) to suppress unreliable local correspondences. Furthermore, we establish VeriRef, a benchmark evaluating exact reference-dependent content recovery and robustness to mismatched references. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves 85.6% character accuracy and 76.3% exact code decoding, compared with 17.4% and 0% for existing methods, while effectively suppressing erroneous reference transfer. It also achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality on CUFED5 and WR-SR.

242. 【2611.33452】HumblePRM: Improving VLM Abstention in Ambiguous Situations through Process Reward Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33452

作者：Saket Reddy,Ke Yang,ChengXiang Zhai

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Vision Language Models, Vision Language, Reliable deployment, deployment of Vision, confidence remain grounded

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reliable deployment of Vision Language Models (VLMs) requires that a model's expressed confidence remain grounded in what it can actually see, and that it recognize when the visual evidence is too ambiguous to commit to a confident response. Standard post-training uses outcome reward models (ORMs) that supervise only the final decision, rewarding a confident answer regardless of whether the image supports one, while existing methods either tend to detect uncertainty post-hoc or assume an objective verdict always exists. We instead split multimodal reasoning into two stages: perception, where the model must factually describe the scene, and calibration, where it must judge whether that evidence is sufficient to commit to a conclusion. We introduce HumblePRM, a lightweight process reward model (PRM) that scores each stage independently, rewarding agreement between evidence and expressed confidence rather than outcome correctness alone. Training with HumblePRM increases abstention due to uncertainty on ambiguous inputs considerably while leaving accuracy on clear images roughly unchanged. We also find that the improvement transfers to Gemma 4 E4B, a larger, popular edge model. Our findings show that process reward supervision is a promising route to VLMs with better calibrated abstention, ensuring more reliability rather than unpredictable behavior.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33439

Abstract:Occluded hazards challenge autonomous driving because visibility timing determines available avoidance actions. Existing scenario generators control hazard motion without jointly specifying exposure and reference-contact times. We propose OnCue, a framework that generates safety-critical encounters in real driving logs with these temporal conditions. Scene geometry provides hidden starts and timed waypoints for pedestrian, cyclist, and vehicle trajectory generation. OnCue demonstrations reduce LatentDriver target collision by 54.8 points on held-out Reactive tests, but reduce overall no-target progress by 12.7 m. These results highlight the value of temporal control for evaluating and improving planners under occlusion.

作者：Zijian Huang,Jiasi Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Visual, identify relevant visual, relevant visual evidence, Visual reasoning requires, derive correct answers

备注： 23 pages, 12 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual reasoning requires models to identify relevant visual evidence and use it to derive correct answers. Outcomebased reinforcement learning (RL) faces two key challenges in this regime: it yields no learning signal when perceptual errors cause all sampled responses to fail, and it broadcasts a uniform advantage across every token within a response. Conversely, while visual self-distillation offers dense guidance from evidence-centered image crops, student learning from token distributions alone often fails to elicit correct final answers. Motivated by an audit of training rollouts that exposes these complementary failure modes, we introduce ViCARD (Visual Credit Assignment with RewardGated Distillation), which integrates per-token visual supervision with verified correctness rewards. For each rollout group, we leverage a teacher with its input image cropped to the relevant visual area, and use this to provide per-token supervision to the student while also valuing correctness. If all sampled rollouts fail, ViCARD samples an additional crop-teacher answer and activates on-policy distillation only if that answer is correct. Together, these mechanisms provide fine-grained visual credit within informative reward groups and a learning signal for otherwise uninformative all-fail groups. Extensive comparisons across model scales demonstrate that ViCARD improves fine-grained visual reasoning performance over RL and distillation baselines, while retaining broad visual reasoning capabilities.

245. 【2611.33425】Anatomy-Aware Pseudo-Labels and Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff Supervision for Semi-Supervised Pancreas Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33425

作者：Alireza Kheyrkhah,Amir Hossein Saleknia,Reza Azad,Sina Houshmand,Dorit Merhof,Ulas Bagci,Alaa Sulaiman

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Confidence-gated co-training assumes, uncertain and concentrated, Confidence-gated co-training, organ boundaries, Dice

备注： 10 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Accepted to WACV 2027 Workshops (FABRIC). \c{opyright} 2027 IEEE

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Confidence-gated co-training assumes that pseudo-label errors are uncertain and concentrated at organ boundaries. We argue that for pancreas CT, where the target organ is a single connected structure and detached fragments are anatomically implausible, the dominant failures arise at three levels: where the organ is, what its boundary should look like, and how a correct boundary is penalized. Anatomical Rectification relabels implausible fragments of a teacher prediction as confident background, without adding any loss or parameter; an audit confirms that its rejections are overwhelmingly detached hallucinations and that thin, tail-like slices are not eroded. Boundary Grafting transplants labelled surface regions with their annotation into unlabeled volumes, replacing unreliable boundary pseudo-labels with ground truth. The residual error is coherent surface displacement, weakly penalized by permutation-invariant Dice and cross-entropy losses. Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff (HAH) supervision anneals a distance-weighted penalty from an annotation-anchored objective that is convex in the prediction to a Hausdorff surrogate, admitting the prediction-dependent distance term only after the predicted surface becomes reliable. On NIH pancreas CT, under the standard semi-supervised split, the full method reaches 84.60% Dice and 4.97 voxels 95HD, improving all four metrics over compared methods; on MSD Task07, 78.90% Dice and 4.19 voxels 95HD. We further contribute PanTS-SSL, a semi-supervised evaluation protocol derived from the public 9,901-case PanTS corpus, with a curated 1,015-volume main pool, labelled tiers of 50, 100 and 250 volumes, an 80-case tumor track, shift probes for acquisition and anatomy change, and a fixed 901-volume test set. On it, the method exceeds the co-training baseline at 100 labels, scales to 85.29% Dice at 250 labels, and reaches 85.50% Dice at 100 labels with HAH.

246. 【2611.33414】Beyond Random Starts: Amortized Initialization for Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33414

作者：Haz Sameen Shahgir,Ali Asif Khan,K. M. Mehemud Azad,Dongwei Lyu,Zhipeng Wei,Rifat Shahriyar,Yufei Li,Xian Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Xi Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：black-box vision-language models, black-box vision-language, Gradient-based transferable adversarial, attacks, adversarial attacks achieve

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Gradient-based transferable adversarial attacks achieve state-of-the-art attack success rates against black-box vision-language models (VLMs). However, they require hundreds of costly optimization steps for each image pair. One possible reason is that these attacks typically start from random perturbations that contain no information about the source or target image. In this work, we study whether amortized adversarial generation can provide better initializations for gradient-based attacks. By learning across many image pairs, amortization allows a generator to produce adversarial perturbations for new inputs without optimizing each perturbation from scratch. We introduce SPARK, a lightweight looped perturbation generator conditioned on both the source and target images. SPARK repeatedly applies the same network to refine a perturbation, producing effective adversarial attacks in a few forward passes without requiring gradients through surrogate vision encoders at inference time. We show that these perturbations provide effective warm starts for existing gradient-based attacks, substantially improving attack success rates while reducing the number of optimization steps required. These improvements generalize across different attack methods, VLMs, and unseen image categories. SPARK also produces effective standalone attacks at a fraction of the computational cost of gradient-based optimization. Code and model weights are available at this https URL.

248. 【2611.33403】ArtKit: Motion-Driven Re-Segmentation and Link Re-Generation for Articulated Objects from RGB-D Video

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33403

作者：Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Vivian Yu,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:An RGB-D camera that films an object being moved, often from one viewpoint, sees one side of it and how its parts move. We present ArtKit, which takes such a video as input to create an articulated model with complete parts, a kinematic tree, and typed joints. Our central idea is generating the hidden geometry and verifying it against what the camera sees. A generative part prior proposes complete, part-decomposed geometry based on the first frame. Point tracks, grouped into rigid trajectories, split and merge the generated parts into links. We then fit revolute and prismatic joints, with a visibility-aware energy ensuring that the articulated model explains the depth observed across the video. A rule checks against the video to choose between synchronous group fits and per-frame chain fits, so that one pipeline serves a diverse range of articulated objects, such as household items and robotic hands. Finally, we regenerate each link of the chosen fit as one part and keep it wherever it agrees better with the observations. We evaluate on Articulate-100 and on HArt, a new benchmark of 60 PartNet-Mobility objects and 8 robotic hands and grippers rendered as RGB-D videos with ground-truth parts and joints. ArtKit reduces the axis and part motion errors of the strongest baseline by more than half on the objects, as well as the axis error on the hands. ArtKit also runs on real recordings and generated videos.

249. 【2611.33399】Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge to Guide Continual Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33399

作者：Shivanand Kundargi,Kowshik Thopalli,Tejas Gokhale

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：data, knowledge acquired, knowledge, continual learning, learning

备注： Published at fifth conference on lifelong learning agents

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The goal of continual learning (CL) is to adapt to new data (plasticity) while retaining the knowledge acquired from old data (stability). Existing methods are explicitly designed to focus on balancing stability and plasticity to mitigate the challenge of catastrophic forgetting while promoting learning. However, the impact of order and nature of samples from the new data that the network is trained on remains an under explored factor. We argue that a CL algorithm should have the ability to rank incoming samples in terms of their relationship with prior data and their effect on the learning process. In this work, we investigate if scoring and prioritizing incoming data based on their semantic relationships with the model's current knowledge can benefit CL. We propose SACK, short for Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge, a plug and play technique for continual learning methods. SACK dissects categorical knowledge of the model into fine-grained concepts, computes the relationships between previously learned concepts and new concepts in each experience, and uses this relationship knowledge for prioritizing new samples. Experiments across several types of CL methods (regularization, replay, and prompt based) in class incremental and task incremental settings demonstrate that integration of SACK not only improves accuracy and reduces forgetting but also handles long tail distribution, helps focus on semantically interpretable regions, and yields better calibrated and robust models compared to prior approaches.

250. 【2611.33392】BreathSplat: Grounding One Smooth Motion in the Breathing Phase for 4D Cone-Beam CT

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33392

作者：Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Four-dimensional cone-beam computed, cone-beam computed tomography, Four-dimensional cone-beam, computed tomography, cone-beam computed

备注： 15 pages, 7 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Four-dimensional cone-beam computed tomography (4D-CBCT) images the anatomy of a breathing patient, but sees each breathing phase from only a few angles. Breathing, however, is a smooth motion that repeats; it has a phase. Existing methods exploit this either by reconstructing the anatomy as a function of the breathing phase, often through bins, or by estimating smooth dynamic motions in time. Yet none of these grounds one smooth motion in the phase. We thus propose to share projections across breathing phases with splines. Specifically, we represent the 4D volume with dynamic Gaussians whose motion uses B-splines in space and time. We ground the motion state in the respiratory phase, which we read from well-studied conventional surrogates. Projections at the same phase in different breathing cycles then constrain the same anatomy and motion. On simulated lung scans, our method, BreathSplat, outperforms recent dynamic methods in reconstruction and motion accuracy, including those given the ground-truth phase. We further show qualitatively, on two real patient scans, that our method provides clearer reconstructions than the state of the art.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Degraded images make vision-language models answer wrongly. Using synthetic paired blur, noise, and low light, we trace which clean image states can still repair those answers from the vision encoder through the language model. 252. 【2611.33387】Synchronizing Cameras via 3D Object Relationships

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33387

作者：Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：dynamic event, synchronize multiple videos, cues, cameras, dynamic

备注： 15 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this paper, we synchronize multiple videos of the same dynamic event through object relationships. Driven by dynamic reconstruction and novel view synthesis, many recent synchronization methods rely on pixel-level evidence, such as point correspondences across views or the photometric losses of a reconstructed scene. In dynamic scenes, however, fast motion and occlusions make the moving regions that carry much of the timing information hard to match across views. We thus propose to bring back the traditional wisdom that objects are reliable cues for global reasoning about cameras. Measured in 3D, the relationships between objects, such as the distance between two people, are stable across all views up to scale; they can serve as cues for alignment. To compare these relationships across cameras, we only need the identity of each object in every view, as the cues are relational. We obtain these identities by giving a vision-language model a few frames of every camera and prompting it to identify the objects in the scene. We then segment and track each identified object through every video with an open-vocabulary segmentor. To obtain the 3D locations of objects, we use a pretrained depth estimator. Where depth or segmentation fails, we complement these 3D cues with 2D motion from point tracks. Finally, we synchronize the cameras by finding the temporal offsets at which the cues of different cameras agree best, first for each camera pair and then for all cameras jointly with robust optimization. We evaluate our method on four standard benchmarks, outperforming the state of the art.

253. 【2611.33382】ArtTet: Watertight Part Meshes of Articulated Objects from Rigid-Body Rules and Partitioned Marching Tetrahedra

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33382

作者：Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multi-view observations, outputs one watertight, part, watertight mesh, mesh

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present ArtTet, a method that reconstructs an articulated object from multi-view observations of two articulation states and outputs one watertight mesh per rigid part together with the joints that move them. Mesh splatting gives explicit triangles, but it treats the object as one scene. It extracts a single global surface and cannot keep the triangles of different parts apart; the mesh it exports is layered, self-intersecting, and full of holes. ArtTet follows a divide-and-conquer strategy instead. We first divide the object into rigid parts with dynamic- and geometric-constrained articulation and ownership inference. A multibody registration of the two states recovers the parts and their screw joints. Rigid-body rules on attachment, contact, and swept motion then correct joint types and part labels without any part annotation. We then divide space into part cells with an articulated tetrahedral partition, and conquer each cell with its own differentiable signed distance field on a shared tetrahedral grid. In these partitioned marching tetrahedra the fields are mutually exclusive by construction, so the extracted part meshes are closed and never overlap. All part meshes are optimized jointly from the images of both states through the recovered joints. On Articulate-100, ArtTet gives the most accurate axis directions, part motions, and part geometry over all objects among methods that use no part masks, and its part meshes have virtually no open boundary edges. We also show reconstructions of real captures from the SplArt dataset.
