备注： 20 pages, 12 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video diffusion and world models generate plausible videos but do not reliably match the requested camera direction and travel distance. We present DreamTour, a training-free framework that generates camera tours from a single image with more accurate control over camera motion. DreamTour first uses a frozen image inpainting prior to extend the photograph beyond its original field of view while preserving the input pixels. Depth-based rendering of this extended scene along the requested camera path produces approximate target views that serve as spatial guidance. We introduce Layout Steering, which aligns early video latents with these views through decaying low-frequency corrections, leaving later denoising to refine appearance and dynamics. For longer tours, we autoregressively generate the video segments, repeating guidance construction from each segment's final frame to maintain camera control across the full trajectory. DreamTour improves directional accuracy and travel calibration on VBench while maintaining competitive video quality. Together, these results demonstrate that training-free spatial guidance enables more accurate and consistent camera control in pretrained video diffusion and world models.

255. 【2611.33374】VersionGS: Compact and Motion-Robust Dynamic Gaussian Splatting via Versioned Gaussians

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33374

作者：Shihang Wei,Yichi Zhang,Fengqing Zhu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recent dynamic Gaussian, Recent dynamic, dynamic Gaussian splatting, encode temporal change, Gaussian splatting methods

备注： 16 pages, 6 figures, 12 tables, including supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent dynamic Gaussian splatting methods encode temporal change, either as smooth functions of time or as constrained updates between frames. We observe that their reconstruction quality degrades significantly under fast motion. In challenging dance sequences, their PSNR drops by 1.1-4.0 dB on high-motion frames compared to low-motion frames. To overcome this limitation, we propose VersionGS, a method that directly encodes per-frame Gaussian states and uses motion estimation only to guide reconstruction. Within each group of frames, unchanged Gaussians keep their current versions in a shared dictionary, while modified Gaussians are appended as new versions. To maximize version reuse, we introduce selective sequential construction, which refines the Gaussians selected by optical flow, photograph differences or rendering errors and leaves the others unchanged. The dictionary is then pruned, fine-tuned with simulated quantization and encoded as images. Adjusting the pruning ratio trades off storage and quality without rebuilding the dictionary. On the fast dance sequences of DanceNet3D, VersionGS improves foreground PSNR by 2.7 dB over the best baseline, and its PSNR decreases by less than 0.3 dB on the highest-motion frames. On N3DV and Technicolor, it requires 6.0x and 5.7x less storage than TAD-GS, the strongest baseline overall, while achieving even higher PSNR. Finally, playback is fast and requires no motion model or neural network, only a 3DGS rasterizer.

Abstract:Expressive style transfer must reproduce a painting's palette, brushwork, and characteristic motifs while retaining the content image's structure. Our analysis across backbones links this gap to how injected features are integrated across neighboring spatial locations. 258. 【2611.33368】Searching Ultra-Long Videos with Decision Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33368

作者：Ce Zhang,Baiqi Li,Seongsu Ha,Gedas Bertasius

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：requires retrieving sparse, extensive video histories, decision model, retrieving sparse, videos requires retrieving

备注： Our code is available at [this https URL](https://github.com/CeeZh/DeLoVi)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Understanding ultra-long videos requires retrieving sparse, query-relevant evidence from extensive video histories. Existing similarity-based retrieval methods are efficient, but they encode the question and the video content independently and thus often miss evidence whose relevance requires reasoning over both. Generative large language models (LLMs) can perform such reasoning, but they typically generate text over multiple rounds of search, which makes each query slow and costly. In this work, we introduce DeLoVi (Decision Models for Long Video), a framework that leverages pretrained decision models to retrieve evidence from ultra-long videos. Like an LLM, a decision model reads the question together with the candidate evidence, so it can reason over both. Unlike an LLM, it directly outputs a relevance score without generating text, which makes it fast and inexpensive. We first present DeLoVi-S, a simple method that generates captions for short video clips and uses a decision model to rank those captions by their relevance to a question. A generative LLM then answers the question using the top-ranked captions. Despite its simplicity, DeLoVi-S achieves state-of-the-art performance on three ultra-long video understanding benchmarks, EgoLifeQA, EgoMemReason, and MM-Lifelong, and generalizes effectively to long-video understanding benchmarks, VideoMME-L and LVBench. However, DeLoVi-S scores every caption for every question, so its cost grows linearly with video length. To reduce this cost, we propose an automated policy evolution framework that iteratively refines the memory construction and retrieval policies around a fixed pretrained decision model. The resulting method, DeLoVi-E, maintains comparable performance while reducing input tokens by 34x on average compared with DeLoVi-S. It also runs over 3x faster than the best prior method on EgoLifeQA while achieving higher accuracy.

259. 【2611.33358】DDxBasis: Explaining Frozen Foundation Encoder-based Medical Classification by Exact Differential Diagnosis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33358

作者：Luojie Huang,Gregory N. McKay,Nicholas J. Durr

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Quantitative Methods (q-bio.QM)

关键词：differential diagnosis, clinician explains, differential, concept, diagnosis

备注： Project page: [this http URL](http://durrlab.github.io/DDxBasis)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In differential diagnosis (DDx), a clinician explains a diagnosis by the few findings that separate it from the alternatives. Medical image classifiers cannot yet explain themselves this way. Existing explanations are either faithful but spread over hundreds of concepts, or compact but leave part of each decision unexplained. We present DDxBasis, which explains a classifier on a frozen foundation encoder as a differential diagnosis in three steps. First, a concept bottleneck reads all patch tokens at every encoder layer using gated, second-order slot attention. A concept basis then regroups the concepts into two-pole axes, each a differential between groups of diagnoses. Finally, we read a differential diagnosis from each axis through the typical presentation of its poles, the pertinent findings of the case, and counterfactual edits of borderline cases. Across five medical datasets, DDxBasis is the most accurate frozen-encoder model and close to the best published domain-specific results. Because every concept adds its own evidence, its reproducible concept basis splits each decision exactly, at no cost in accuracy. Without ordered stage or concept labels, the axes recover cell lineages and rare subtypes, agree with dermatologists' structure annotations, and expose planted and real shortcuts.

260. 【2611.33344】LGCount: Layout-Guided Diffusion for Zero-Shot Object Counting

作者：Adriano D'Alessandro,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Ghassan Hamarneh

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Class-agnostic counting requires the user to specify what to count, usually with a class name or a few annotated boxes. We propose a richer text-based specification: a structured layout that names the target, the region that holds it, the distractors to ignore. A prompt upsampler writes this layout from the image and the requested category alone, and an image-editing diffusion model, adapted into a single-step counter, turns it into a density map. A single photograph can be counted by category, by region, or by granularity without retraining.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model's weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.

262. 【2611.33331】Do Not Let the Prior Heal the Anomaly: Selective Anchoring for Detection from Incomplete Measurements

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33331

作者：Nicolas Tricard,Stefan Brandstätter,Jason Chen,Zituo Chen,Linzheng Wang,Georg Langs,Sili Deng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Medical Physics (physics.med-ph)

关键词：recover anatomical detail, incomplete medical measurements, aims to detect, recover anatomical, anatomical detail

备注： 26 pages, 7 figures, 23 tables. Includes supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative priors can recover anatomical detail from incomplete medical measurements, but may erase the very lesions that imaging aims to detect, even when the measurements contain evidence of them. We introduce selective anchoring that protects reliably measured image components from prior-driven changes, while allowing expressive generative models to reconstruct unresolved structure. Acquisition uncertainty in the protected components is either added deterministically to the downstream test or carried by the ensemble through stochastic perturbations of the readings. We implement this principle as a wrapper for pretrained diffusion and flow samplers and as AnchoredFM, a fast constrained flow model. Under a linear-Gaussian measurement model, the protected coefficients are unbiased in expectation and achieve nominal interval coverage across repeated acquisitions, independently of the learned prior. Across sparse-view CT, accelerated brain MRI, and photoacoustic tomography, selective anchoring improves lesion contrast preservation and uncertainty reliability, and can reduce reconstruction error within lesions. For tumor detection in accelerated MRI, with a healthy reference estimated from the measurements alone, deterministic anchoring raises mean AUROC across eight samplers from 78.4% to 84.6% and lesion sensitivity at 0.25 false-positive marks per slice from 38.8% to 47.5%, with gains in all four predefined tumor-size groups. At thresholds frozen on healthy validation data, mean sensitivity rises from 20.2% to 44.1% (37.6% with stochastic anchoring). These findings show how expressive generative priors can reconstruct unresolved anatomy without healing away the abnormalities that the measurements support.

263. 【2611.33323】StyleID++: Position-Decoupled and Structure-Coherent Style Retrieval across Diffusion Architectures

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33323

作者：Chun Xie,Itaru Kitahara

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Attention injection offers, remarkably simple route, Attention injection, pretrained diffusion model, injection offers

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Attention injection offers a remarkably simple route to training-free style transfer: content queries retrieve style keys and values inside a pretrained diffusion model. We show that this operation also creates an overlooked failure mode. Position signals that are useful within one image become a spurious shared coordinate system across two spatially unrelated images, causing content tokens to favor style tokens at similar coordinates. This cross-image positional leakage appears in both convolutional U-Nets and diffusion transformers (DiTs), despite their different position mechanisms. Removing the positional shortcut exposes a second problem: unconstrained global retrieval lets related content tokens select incompatible style regions, producing fragmented appearance. We present StyleID++, a training-free framework that addresses the two problems jointly. Position-Decoupled Retrieval reduces coordinate-dependent matching through circular padding in U-Nets and injection-only RoPE removal in DiTs, while preserving native position modeling elsewhere. Affinity-Coherent Refinement then regularizes style assignments with the content self-attention graph, encouraging structurally related tokens to retrieve compatible style evidence. We quantify the leakage with a coordinate--centroid correlation and a permutation test on the style reference. The permutation error can be driven to zero in both backbones, which identifies the position mechanism as the carrier of the leakage, and StyleID++ obtains the best ArtFID of all methods we compare.

264. 【2611.33316】What Does RL Post-Training Teach Text-to-Image Models? A Skill-Wise Analysis of Flow-GRPO

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33316

作者：Cheng Zhang,Jiesong Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：learned compositional generation, Reinforcement learning, base samples, base, Flow-GRPO lifts

备注： 19 pages, 8 figures, 25 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become a standard way to improve text-to-image flow models: Flow-GRPO lifts the GenEval score of SD3.5-M from 0.63 to 0.95, a gain read as the model having learned compositional generation. We ask how much of that gain the base model's own samples already contained, by measuring pass@k over shared noise seeds, one skill at a time, before and after RL, on prompts never used in training. The answer differs by skill. On four of GenEval's six skills, where 32 base samples already solve almost every prompt, the gain is sharpening: one post-trained sample is worth 4 to 10 base samples, and pass@32 barely moves. On spatial relations it is unlocking: pass@32 rises from 0.69 to 1.00, 128 base samples do not match one post-trained sample, and prompts the base model never solved in 32 samples are solved 93% of the time, as often as those it solved. The benchmark average, 31 base samples per post-trained sample, describes no skill. Relations carry 35% of the gain; of the other 65%, selecting among 8 base samples with the judge behind the reward recovers nine tenths. We propose an account of when each occurs: within a prompt, a group-relative update re-weights only the outcomes its samples contain, and a flow policy cannot widen the noise that produces them; across prompts, shared parameters let successes on some prompts repair a deficit common to many. The same anatomy applies to a text-rendering checkpoint. The account's predictions are tested with a checkpoint trained without KL, within-prompt diversity, and controlled training that holds object classes out, where sharpening comes first and transfers, and unlocking follows, on practised objects before unseen ones.

265. 【2611.33315】Large-scale Model Merging via Recovery-aware Task Weighting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33315

作者：Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Boming Chen,Martin Renqiang Min,Shihao Ji

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Model, Recovery-aware Task Weighting, multiple task-specific expert, merging, tasks

备注： 24 pages, including appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Model merging combines multiple task-specific expert models into a single model. However, merging preserves expert capabilities unevenly across tasks. This imbalance is particularly pronounced in large-scale settings, where some tasks can suffer severe capability losses even when average accuracy remains high. To address this imbalance, we introduce Recovery-aware Task Weighting (RaTW), a model merging framework that assigns larger weights to tasks whose expert capabilities are poorly retained by an initial merged model. We quantify these recovery opportunities as the fraction of a task's labeled validation samples that its expert predicts correctly but the initial merged model gets wrong, and use the resulting task weights to re-merge the experts. To evaluate merging at large scale, we construct CLIPMerge-100, a 100-task benchmark with task-specific experts on CLIP ViT-L/14 and ViT-B/32. Across eleven merging methods with 20, 50, and 100 tasks on two CLIP backbones, RaTW improves both mean accuracy and tail20 (the mean expert-normalized accuracy of the 20% worst-retained tasks) in all 33 ViT-L/14 settings and 32 of 33 ViT-B/32 settings. We further introduce ReForge++, which combines RaTW with iterative model refinement and outperforms all evaluated baselines, including a matched-budget control, in both mean accuracy and tail20 across all settings.

266. 【2611.33308】Change Partners at Every Layer: Balanced Random Regrouping for Model Merging

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33308

作者：Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Shihao Ji

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：substantially reduces accuracy, Model merging combines, combines fine-tuned experts, merging combines fine-tuned, Model

备注： 26 pages, including appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Model merging combines fine-tuned experts into one model that serves many tasks without retraining, but merging many experts this way substantially reduces accuracy. Grouping offers a middle ground: at every layer or weight matrix, the $N$ experts are divided into $KN$ groups, the experts in a group are merged into one shared module, and a task uses the module merged from its expert's group. Natural choices are to keep the same partners across layers or to group similar experts. Surprisingly, we find that randomly changing partners at every layer outperforms both. We call this Balanced Random Regrouping (BRR): it independently partitions the experts into $K$ equal-sized random groups at each layer (BRR-layer) or weight matrix (BRR-matrix) and merges each group with an existing method. With validation-selected merge scales, both BRR variants outperform reusing one grouping across layers in all 24 CLIP settings, by 1.0-6.8 points, and BRR-matrix also outperforms the similarity-based FlexMerge in every setting. A first-order analysis explains this gain. At each layer, merging a task's expert with its partners shifts the task's output features; when the partners are the same at every layer, these shifts tend to point in similar directions and add up, whereas with new partners at every layer they vary independently and add up less on average. A series of experiments supports this analysis, which further guides a grouping search that can outperform BRR.

267. 【2611.33304】he Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33304

作者：Ross Murphy,Gabriel Brostow

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Platonic Representation Hypothesis, Representation Hypothesis, Platonic Representation, model J-space, language model J-space

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

268. 【2611.33296】CoSMo: A Compact Spectral Space of Body Motion in Video

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33296

作者：Maham Tanveer,Hao Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：compact spectral space, scaled in intensity, intensity and detail, sampled anew, spectral space

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://cosmospectral.github.io/cosmo/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We introduce CoSMo, a compact spectral space of body motion in video, in which each motion is a single code that can be compared and edited without training, transferred to a new subject, interpolated, composed, scaled in intensity and detail, or sampled anew. Our encoder expresses how a clip's DINOv2 features change over time in a fixed temporal basis, the discrete cosine transform (DCT), and compresses this change into 384 numbers. Because each frequency band is the same cosine pattern in every code, codes are aligned in time, ordered from coarse to fine, and can be blended or added without matching frames. What stays constant over a clip cancels, and keeping fewer frequencies leaves less of the performer and scene. The encoder is trained on real videos and on synthetic motions of several characters, and lightweight adapters let a frozen image-to-video model render any code with any subject. On unseen videos, our code ranks the same motion on other bodies higher than DisMo, a per-frame representation, while its nearest neighbors show the same performer about 40% less often with a seven times more compact code. In generation, without per-motion training, CoSMo transfers motion more faithfully than FlexiAct and a text-prompt baseline, at least as faithfully as DisMo on automatic metrics and clearly better between humanoid bodies, and its blends keep much more of both source motions than DisMo's. Users rank CoSMo first nearly twice as often as DisMo for motion transfer (59% vs. 30%), place it top-two 94% of the time, and prefer it in 76% of the interpolation comparisons.

Abstract:Discrete visual tokens encode images for reconstruction and serve as training targets for autoregressive image generators. Vector-quantized tokenizers typically assign each encoder feature its nearest code independently. The decoder uses these codes jointly, so a sequence of individually nearest codes can reconstruct an image less faithfully than an alternative assignment. 272. 【2611.33252】MonoQPM: Splitting Features into Concepts for Inherent Interpretability and Predictive Performance

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33252

作者：Thomas Norrenbrock,Robert Zimmermann,Timo Kaiser,Sovan Biswas,Neslihan Kose,Bodo Rosenhahn

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：deep neural networks, achieving interpretable deep, interpretable deep neural, Programming Enhanced Model, Quadratic Programming Enhanced

备注： Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (10/2026). OpenReview: [this https URL](https://openreview.net/forum?id=8bQNRlFQhZ)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Alongside superposition, polysemanticity is one of the primary obstacles to achieving interpretable deep neural networks. While these phenomena are typically intrinsically linked, the Quadratic Programming Enhanced Model (QPM) naturally decouples them. By representing classes with a sparse binary assignment of very few features, QPM prevents superposition by design on its final features but still exhibits polysemanticity. However, measuring polysemanticity is an open problem. This work proposes a utility-focused approach to measuring polysemanticity by quantifying the decrease in interference-induced activation errors, which yields the practical utility of tighter prediction sets using Conformal Prediction (CP). We explicitly disentangle the polysemantic features of QPM into monosemantic concepts to create the Monosemantic QPM (MonoQPM). Because its features are disentangled, MonoQPM acts as a significantly more efficient Conformal Predictor. Additionally, we introduce CUBCars, a dataset providing ground truth information about shared concepts. Using this and other datasets, we demonstrate that polysemanticity emerges in QPM across all tested architectures, but is effectively alleviated by MonoQPM. For instance, MonoQPM guarantees 88% coverage using Adaptive Prediction Sets on ImageNet with just 66% of the frozen QPM's set size.

273. 【2611.33232】How Many Samples Is RL Worth? Best-of-N versus RL Post-Training for Text-to-Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33232

作者：Salil Bhatnagar

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Reinforcement learning, base model, checked automatically, common last step, base

备注： 38 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) on a reward that can be checked automatically has become a common last step in the training of text-to-image models. A simpler way to get more correct images is best-of-N: draw N images from the base model and keep the one that a picker prefers. We ask how many base model samples one RL sample is worth, and call this number the exchange rate. We measure it for Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium and its public Flow-GRPO checkpoint on 398 new prompts in the style of GenEval, the benchmark behind the RL reward, with human labels on every prompt and two automatic judges other than the reward. The answer depends on the prompt. Four fifths of the prompts do not ask for a position (for example, one object left of another). On these, the base model catches up: best-of-16 with an off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM) as the picker is about as good as one RL image. A person who looks through up to 3 base images finds a correct one as often as one RL image is correct. On the prompts that ask for a position, RL is far ahead: a person finds a correct image among 8 base images for only 52% of the prompts, against 92% for one RL image. Over all prompts, our primary test, which we fixed in advance, finds best-of-16 with the VLM picker 9.3 points behind one RL image when both automatic judges must pass an image. People see a smaller gap, 5.5 and 6.5 points in two checks. FLUX.1-dev shows a similar gap in the same test. RL also solves 20 prompts that the base model misses in 128 tries, and 1,024 tries recover half of them. The automatic judges are wrong in opposite directions: a detector-based judge like the reward, as used in our primary test, reports a larger gap than people see, and VLM judges report little or none. Comparisons of RL with best-of-N should therefore check the selected images by hand.

Abstract:Face Verification (FV) systems have exhibited remarkable performance in verification tasks and have consequently garnered extensive adoption across various applications, from identity duplication to authentication in mobile payments. 275. 【2611.33210】Confidence-Based Adaptive Retrieval Fails Where It Matters Most: A Paradox in Biomedical Question Answering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33210

作者：Chengying She,Siyu Deng,Haiyan Ge,Lizhuang Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Large language models, plausible incorrect answers, incorrect answers remain, Large language, plausible incorrect

备注： accepted by BIBM2027 Workshop

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for biomedical question answering, but plausible incorrect answers remain a safety risk in clinical settings. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can ground responses in medical literature, while confidence-based adaptive RAG retrieves only when the model appears uncertain. We evaluate this routing strategy on three biomedical QA benchmarks of increasing difficulty and compare models across a broad range of parameter scales. The results reveal a paradox: confidence is well calibrated (AUROC 0.78--0.89) on standard medical exam questions where retrieval adds little value (+2.4\% to -1.4\%), yet becomes \emph{near-random} (AUROC 0.506) on specialist-level questions where retrieval helps most (+11.2\%). Five post-hoc calibration correction strategies, including density-ratio reweighting and rank-preserving transformation, do not recover a useful routing signal on hard questions (best AUROC: 0.507). Cross-model comparison shows that larger parameter scale and higher base accuracy do not guarantee reliable confidence-based routing under specialist-domain difficulty shift. These findings challenge the premise of confidence-based adaptive RAG: in high-stakes biomedical settings, defaulting to always-retrieve is safer and more accurate than confidence-based routing when calibration on the target domain is unknown.

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Agentic benchmarks report, Agentic benchmarks, wrong order, benchmarks report, report a success

备注： 13 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic benchmarks report a success rate that confounds distinct causes of failure: misreading the environment, never working out how components of the environment operates, operating the components in the wrong order, and failing to recover from a mistake, each of which calls for a different fix. We present an early preview of MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld - an interactive maze environment built for attribution rather than ranking. Four different types of mechanisms, namely key-door pairs, switch-gate pairs, distractors and decoys, and ordered combinations of these mechanisms, can be added, modified, or removed independently while the renderer, the action space, and the scoring function stay fixed. A breadth-first search over the joint state of position, facing direction, inventory, and mechanism state gives every instance an exact optimal action count, which sets its difficulty and grades unfinished episodes. We fix the evaluation protocol on 15 held-out mazes over 540 episodes, then evaluate three vision-language models on 50 mazes. They solve 6 of 150 episodes, 45 of the 50 mazes were not solved by any model, and no episode containing a switch-gate mechanism was solved.

280. 【2611.30254】Innata-0: A Native Embodied World System with Model-Agent Co-Design

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30254

作者：Innata Team:Yu Bai,Ziyi Bai,Yuxuan Cai,Chenrui He,Wenhao Li,Zhe Lin,Heng Wang,Hai Yuan,Zhiwei Yu,Yuanhang Zhang,Luyou Chen,Tiezhu Gao,Zheng Geng,Jinbang Guo,Dongxu Han,Diqi He,Börje F. Karlsson,Jintao Li,Zhiyu Li,Xiongkun Linghu,Guang Liu,You Liu,Zhen Luo,Mengsi Lyu,Yufeng Lyu,Zheng Lyu,Quanyue Ma,Chunlei Men,Ziqiang Ni,Tengfei Pan,Yijun Shen,Liang Sun,Shaoxuan Xie,Xiang Yang,Guocai Yao,Hongjun Zhang,Yingli Zhao,Yonghua Lin,Zhongyuan Wang,Huan Yang

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：partially observable, physical world, physical, world, embodied

备注： 87 pages, 25 figures, Project Page: [this https URL](https://baai-eai.github.io/innata-0/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The physical world is open-ended, dynamic, and partially observable. While foundation models have advanced embodied intelligence, standalone models struggle with long-horizon execution, and simply integrating models with agents may lead to inconsistencies between planning and physical control. To support reliable and adaptive behaviors in the physical world, we introduce Innata-0, a native embodied world system. Innata-0 jointly designs an embodied world-action model and an agent to combine their complementary strengths. The model, Innata-0-WAM, adopts a tri-tower Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that integrates understanding, prediction, and action generation. The agent, Innata-0-Agent, coordinates long-horizon execution through memory, verification, and recovery. A co-design protocol is further proposed to align model training with agent execution and leverage validated experiences to support subsequent adaptation and model improvement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate strong embodied capabilities across understanding, world prediction, and robotic manipulation. Innata-0 represents our first step toward physical intelligence, and we see it as the beginning of a much longer journey.

281. 【2611.30173】Confidently Lost: Silent Failure and Edge Deployment of Dense Visual SLAM under Photon Starvation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30173

作者：Shrikrishna Bhagirath Rajule,Srinath Neelamagam,Aniket Bera

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Dense visual SLAM, visual SLAM built, Dense visual, visual SLAM, SLAM built

备注： 8 pages, 7 figures. Submitted to IEEE ICRA 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dense visual SLAM built on learned two-view geometry is evaluated in good light; in low light it fails silently. Two frames of 9-level quantized noise, no scene at all, give MASt3R-SLAM's network a cross-view geometric self-consistency of 0.936; a real dark frame pair gives 0.935. Starved of structure, the network invents the same geometry for both views and the system trusts the agreement: on a dark sequence it consumes 1,605 frames, emits 7 keyframes, rejects no factor-graph edges and reports full frame rate, while over 95% of the correspondences it accepts violate the ground-truth epipolar geometry. One inflated statistic is read by two subsystems against thresholds in opposite directions, so keyframing starves while relocalization accepts almost every candidate. The same statistic, read at keyframe-pair baselines, separates the confident collapse from working runs on the ETH3D dark sequences without ground truth or extra computation (in-sample). Low-light-adapted weights take tracked trajectories from 1/6 to 5/6, as does retuning two thresholds; neither yields accurate ones, because the adaptation withdraws confidence from wrong geometry rather than finding right geometry. Replayed on a Jetson AGX Orin carried by a legged robot, the mechanism's signature reproduces on the two darkest of four routes, and which of its faces appears varies with route geometry and sensor encoding. The network does not lose confidence in the dark; it manufactures it.

282. 【2611.34607】Blind spatially varying PSF estimation for fluorescence microscopy

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34607

作者：Minh Hai Nguyen,Florian Sarron,Paul Escande,Sylvain Cantaloube,Thomas Mangeat,Pierre Weiss

类目：Optics (physics.optics); Computational Engineering, Finance, and Science (cs.CE); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：point spread function, underlying point spread, requires dedicated acquisitions, aberrations degrade fluorescence, degrade fluorescence microscopy

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Optical aberrations degrade fluorescence microscopy images and measuring the underlying point spread function (PSF) often requires dedicated acquisitions or isolated emitters. Here we introduce DeepBlur, a neural network that estimates spatially varying two-dimensional PSFs directly from single images of biological structures. Trained entirely on physical simulations spanning aberrations, illumination and noise, it adapts to diverse imaging conditions without requiring user-supplied optical parameters. Using a deformable mirror, we acquire 1,290 images and assess blind PSF estimates against independent references. DeepBlur estimates agree closely with these references and improve deconvolution of experimentally aberrated images. Local PSF estimation also enables correction of spatially varying defocus and improves random-illumination super-resolution reconstruction in biological tissue. Available through DeepInv, a napari plugin and a web demonstration, DeepBlur can be readily integrated into computational microscopy workflows.

283. 【2611.34230】Weakly Conditioned Diffusion Model Augmentation for Prostate Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34230

作者：Chibike Ugbam,Muhammad Bilal,Jia Wu,Shafaq Murad,Rizwan Qureshi,Hazrat Ali

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Deep learning models, Deep learning, limited heterogeneous data, prostate MRI segmentation, struggle to generalize

备注： 15 pages, preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep learning models for prostate MRI segmentation often struggle to generalize when trained on limited heterogeneous data, leading to boundary errors and over-segmentation. We present an augmentation framework using mask-conditioned synthetic and diverse T2-weighted MRI volumes to improve boundary precision and reduce over-segmentation. We employ a 2.5D weakly mask-conditioned denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) via channel concatenation. The diffusion model conditions on stacks of adjacent binary masks from training folds. The model generates T2 volumes consistent with the conditioning anatomy while diversifying tissue appearance. We evaluate the proposed approach on two cohorts of data: (i) the LUND probe cohort (n=432) for strategy selection and training nnU-Net from scratch with a 23% synthetic data mix, and (ii) the Medical Segmentation Decathlon (MSD) prostate cohort (n=32) evaluated with a 20% synthetic data mix. For the LUND cohort, we found that training an nnU-Net model from scratch with a 23% synthetic data mix reduced the mean false-positive voxels per case from 11,102 (baseline) to 10,757. On the MSD cohort, the augmented model with a 20% synthetic mix achieved a mean cross-validated Dice of 0.9094, improving over the baseline (0.8935) by 1.59 percentage points and reducing mean false-positive voxels by 23.1% (4,201 to 3,229). Experimental results on both these cohorts suggest that mask-conditioned DDPM augmentation can reduce over-segmentation errors and improve boundary adherence in data-constrained settings.

284. 【2611.33839】PRISM: Perceptual Rate-Controllable Extreme Image Compression with Visual State-Space Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33839

作者：Md Adnan Faisal Hossain,Fengqing Zhu

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Generative codecs built, pretrained diffusion priors, diffusion priors achieve, priors achieve high, achieve high perceptual

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative codecs built on pretrained diffusion priors achieve high perceptual quality at ultra-low bitrates (below 0.05 bpp). However, most methods still require a separate model for each bitrate, and offer no control over the amount of detail synthesized. Their rate efficiency also depends on the entropy model, where Mamba-based state-space models (SSMs) have recently improved autoregressive context modeling. However, conventional SSM scans are not aligned with the decoding order, and each decoding pass restarts from an empty state, discarding context accumulated in previous passes. We propose PRISM, an extreme image compression method that addresses these limitations. A single model provides two continuous controls: a rate knob that traverses the rate-distortion curve and a realism knob that adjusts the level of detail synthesized. Its visual state-space (VSS) context model introduces a decode-order-aligned (DOA) scan, which predicts each latent from a state that summarizes all decoded context, and cross-tap state carry, which initializes each tap with a summary of the previous one. Experiments show that PRISM outperforms both variable-rate and fixed-rate codecs, requiring on average 22% less bitrate than the strongest baseline at equal FID while also achieving higher PSNR.

285. 【2611.33805】SpiderSkel: Sub-Pixel Multi-Tangent 2D Skeletonization from Inscribed-Disc Geometry

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33805

作者：Zhexian Zhou,Lian Ren,Xiao Feng,Sebastian Scherer

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：pixel-grid discretization perturbs, boundary-contact geometry underlying, compact medial representation, creating tension, tension among connectivity

备注： 13 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables, plus 6 pages of supplementary material. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Image Processing

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Skeletonization reduces a binary shape to a compact medial representation, but pixel-grid discretization perturbs the boundary-contact geometry underlying the medial axis, creating tension among connectivity, reconstruction fidelity, and skeleton compactness. SpiderSkel is a family of training-free two-dimensional skeletonization methods built on multi-tangent inscribed-disc geometry. A tie-aware exact Euclidean distance transform retains tied nearest boundary sites rather than collapsing them to a single label; medial candidates are localised to sub-pixel positions from the inscribed disc's tangent contacts, filtered by the angular span of those contacts, and completed by connectivity-aware refinement. Across nineteen public benchmarks comprising 11,323 images of shapes, retinal vessels, cracks and roads, and document images, a single operating point fixed across datasets yields skeletons that, on every image, have exact skeleton topology under the component/hole certificate, remain fully connected, and have exact per-object reconstruction topology. The three SpiderSkel variants are the only evaluated methods to satisfy all three properties across every category; within each category, every variant is Pareto-optimal with respect to all twenty-two baselines in strict sub-pixel reconstruction Dice versus reduction rate. Incremental engines for all three variants reproduce the skeleton of a complete recomputation exactly across 23,040 verified edit steps, with median update latencies of 15.4-18.1 ms per foreground edit and 4.3-4.4 ms per canvas edit, and speedups reaching 33.0-fold on canvas edits.

286. 【2611.33632】Interpreting Speckle Effects Through Neuron Interventions

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33632

作者：Shubham K. Singh,Hare Krishna

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：multiplicative noise inherent, synthetic aperture radar, pretrained SAR models, aperture radar, reduces the performance

备注： 17 Pages 7 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Speckle, the multiplicative noise inherent to synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imaging, reduces the performance of pretrained SAR models. We use interpretability methods to examine its effects inside pretrained vision models. We identify neurons that respond selectively to speckle and test whether correcting their activations improves reconstruction. We study SARMAE, an Earth observation model pretrained on a million-scale SAR dataset. Synthetic scenes with known reflectivity allow us to measure recovery precisely. The model contains 38 speckle-selective neurons that track speckle's value distribution. Restoring these neurons to their clean values does not improve reconstruction. Activation patching across layers shows that the first MLP has the largest restoration and injection effects. Within this layer, a different set of 64 neurons recovers 44.1\% of the correlation loss on held-out scenes. When the speckle-selective neurons are held at their noisy values, 96.5\% of this gain remains, while holding the values of three decoder heads removes 37.4\%. In an ImageNet-pretrained MAE, restoration is most effective in the third MLP block. On manipulated Sentinel-1 and Capella images, the same 64 neurons also improve reconstruction, whereas for land-cover classification, only restoring the entire first MLP yields a clear gain. The neurons that respond most to speckle are therefore not the ones the model uses to recover the scene.

287. 【2611.33309】NeuroRefine: Six-Head MLP Content Conditioning and Qwen Refinement for Faithful fMRI Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33309

作者：Runqi Xu,Vince D. Calhoun

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：frequently mispaints colour, low-level branch outlines, diffusion models, diffusion model, lossy bottleneck

备注： 22 pages, 13 figures, 1 table

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reconstructing seen images from fMRI has advanced through diffusion models, but the current state-of-the-art Brain-IT pipeline still has a lossy bottleneck: its low-level branch outlines the figure well, yet the diffusion model that follows frequently mispaints colour, object identity and layout. We move one step backward and condition the refiner directly on the fMRI-derived low-level image, using six one-hidden-layer MLP heads (scene, main-subject category, count, colour, object, layout) to predict the content prompt that guides a frozen Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 model. On the 930 shared NSD concepts, scored with Brain-IT's own metric code on the same ground truth, NeuroRefine reaches a CLIP image cosine of 0.755 against 0.735 for Brain-IT enhanced and produces markedly sharper images, while the saturated 2-way identification is unchanged. The low-level image supplies an explicit spatial prior rather than a lossless layout: refinement trades some layout agreement (0.541 against 0.638) for semantic fidelity and texture. We then show that the standard panel is multi-factor: a high PixCorr can coexist with completely different content, so we read the low-level and semantic factors separately and quantify where they disagree. Finally, we trace the cortical sources of the prompt heads across subjects and report their causal lesion profile and the fMRI noise ceiling. Direction errors are left to future work.

288. 【2611.28905】Beyond the section plane: Robust 3D reconstruction of multiplex immunofluorescence exposes limitations of 2D spatial cell statistics

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28905

作者：Junming Ma(1),Olle Edgren Schüllerqvist(1 and 2),Hui Yu(3),Carina Strell(3),Patrick Micke(3),Joakim Lindblad(1 and 2),Artur Mezheyeuski(4),Nataša Sladoje(1 and 2) ((1) Department of Information Technology, Uppsala University, Uppsala, Sweden, (2) SciLifeLab, Uppsala University, Uppsala, Sweden, (3) Department of Immunology, Genetics and Pathology, Uppsala University, Uppsala, Sweden, (4) Vall d'Hebron Institute of Research Institute of Oncology, Barcelona, Spain)

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：remain largely unquantified, spatial cell statistics, bias spatial cell, single thin tissue, multiplex immunofluorescence

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spatial analysis of the tumour microenvironment with multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) typically relies on single thin tissue sections, which misrepresent the 3D tissue organisation and bias spatial cell statistics in ways that remain largely unquantified. We reconstruct 24 breast and lung tumour tissue microarray cores in 3D, each from 15-20 serial mIF sections, and compare spatial cell statistics in 3D with those from individual sections. Our pipeline combines affine pre-alignment with dense deformable refinement using RoMa v2, achieving a median target registration error of 1.79 $\mu$m, well below nuclear size, which allows nuclei to be linked across sections. While cell-type proportions are preserved in 2D (Pearson corr. $r \geq 0.99$), 3D analysis reveals up to 3.8 times shorter nearest-neighbour distances, 13-19% higher neighbourhood entropy, and 2-4 times more contacts per cell. Tissue composition can thus be reliably estimated from individual sections, whereas cell-cell interactions require volumetric analysis.
